https://www.math.columbia.edu/~chaoli/slides/Nelson.pdf, The Orbit Method and Automorphic L-Functions. Paul Nelson's slides on microlocal analysis on reductive groups, coadjoint orbits, Kirillov character formulas, branching coefficients, and the subconvexity problem.

https://tdejong.com/mhe60/slides/coquand.pdf, Sheaf Models in Type Theory. Thierry Coquand's slides on constructive sheaf models of choice sequences and univalence.

https://www.sci.unich.it/geodeep2022/slides/GDL_SummerSchool_Part2.pdf, Sheaf Theory in Machine Learning. Introduction to cellular sheaves, presheaves, and discrete Laplacians on graphs for data science.

https://www.fields.utoronto.ca/talk-media/1/45/37/slides.pdf, Stacks of Shtukas and Geometric Langlands. Slides on the geometric Langlands correspondence for function fields using stacks of shtukas and local Shimura varieties.

https://members.loria.fr/EThome/teaching/2022-cse-291-14/slides/cse-291-14-lecture-04.pdf, Number Fields and Function Fields Analogy. Algorithmic aspects of the analogy between number rings $\mathcal{O}_K$ and function field curves $\mathbb{F}_q[C]$.

https://intrepid-math.com/simple_words/2024/Slides/Slides%20-%20Hanson.pdf, Introductory Number Fields. Elementary slides introducing algebraic integers, norms, and ideal factorizations.

https://www.fields.utoronto.ca/talk-media/1/50/58/slides.pdf, Surreal Numbers and Combinatorial Games. Introduction to Conway's surreal numbers as a totally ordered field constructed via transfinite game trees.

https://math-sites.uncg.edu/sites/number-theory/summerschool/2016/riemann-roch-slides.pdf, Function Fields and Curves. Summary of valuations, places, divisors, and the Riemann–Roch theorem for algebraic function fields.

https://www.math.fsu.edu/~hoeij/papers/2015/slides.pdf, Valuations and Places of Function Fields. Slides detailing discrete valuation rings, places, and the integral closure of curves.

https://mathtube.org/sites/default/files/lecture-extra-files/Slides-NT%2520Seminar%2520%28UofL%29-Feb2024-Abbas.pdf, History of the Hilbert Class Field. Slides tracing the history of class field theory from Weber and Hilbert to Takagi and Artin.

https://math.mit.edu/~poonen/papers/curves.pdf, Curves over Local and Global Fields. Bjorn Poonen's comprehensive survey on the arithmetic of curves, Jacobian varieties, and rational points.

https://www.mathunion.org/fileadmin/IMU/ICM2022/Presentation-slides/54-Joseph%20Silverman.pdf, Arithmetic Dynamics by Joseph Silverman. ICM survey on heights, dynamical moduli spaces, and the arithmetic of rational maps.

http://people.math.harvard.edu/~lurie/282ynotes/LectureXXI-Verdier.pdf, Verdier Duality and Cosheaves. Jacob Lurie's notes on cosheaves with compact support, the equivalence $\operatorname{Shv}(X)^\vee \cong \operatorname{CoShv}(X)$, and Poincaré–Verdier duality.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0212266, Introduction to Gerbes and Non-Abelian Cohomology. Ieke Moerdijk's notes defining gerbes as stacks of non-empty, connected groupoids, classified by non-abelian Čech 2-cocycles $\check{H}^2(X, \mathcal{G})$.

https://www.ams.org/journals/notices/202301/rnoti-p140.pdf, The Isbell Adjunction by John Baez. Expository article on the duality between geometry (spaces) and algebra (functions) via the Isbell adjunction and the Yoneda/co-Yoneda lemmas.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0607182, Fontaine Theory and Number-Field Analogies. Systematic dictionary comparing function fields over $\mathbb{F}_q$ and number fields through $p$-adic Hodge theory and Fontaine's period rings.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/xnotes/TateCW.pdf, The Work of John Tate. Milne's survey of Tate's contributions, including Tate's thesis, the Tate conjecture, $p$-divisible groups, and Galois cohomology.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/xnotes/Shimura.pdf, The Work of Goro Shimura. Milne's overview of Shimura's contributions to complex multiplication, modular forms, and Shimura varieties.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2501.13229, Simplicial Type Theory and Synthetic Higher Categories. Formalization of the Yoneda lemma, Segal conditions, and Rezk types in simplicial homotopy type theory.

https://amathew.wordpress.com/2011/10/10/brown-representability-and-infinity-categories/#more-2907, Brown Representability in Higher Categories. Akhil Mathew's exposition on the Brown representability theorem for stable $\infty$-categories and generalized cohomology functors.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2309.08340, The Yoneda Lemma in Homotopy Type Theory. Formalization of $\infty$-categories, right fibrations, covariance, and the $\infty$-categorical Yoneda lemma using synthetic type theory.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/Makkai-GabrielUlmerDuality.pdf, Gabriel-Ulmer Duality Notes. Slides on the 2-adjunction between finitely complete categories $\mathbf{LEX}^{\text{op}}$ and locally finitely presentable categories $\mathbf{LFP}$.

https://www.math.ias.edu/~lurie/278xnotes/Lecture10-Giraud.pdf, Giraud's Axioms for Topoi. Jacob Lurie's lecture notes characterizing Grothendieck topoi as left-exact accessible localizations of presheaf categories.

https://mathoverflow.net/questions/293031/gabriel-ulmer-duality-for-infty-categories, Gabriel-Ulmer Duality for Infinity-Categories. Denis Nardin's proof factoring Gabriel–Ulmer duality for $\infty$-categories via ind-completion $\operatorname{Ind}(-)$ and filtered colimits.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2308.11967, Duality of Theories and Models. Gabriel–Ulmer duality viewed as a natural equivalence between small finite-limit categories (theories) and locally presentable categories (models).

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0509111, Tannakian Categories of Flat Connections. Construction of neutral Tannakian categories of vector bundles with flat connections and their relations to the étale fundamental group.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1204.5787, Tannaka Duality over Ring Spectra. Developing Galois categories and neutral Tannakian duality over structured ring spectra, identifying affine group schemes with spectral fundamental groups.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/download/etale-cohomology.pdf, Étale Cohomology in the Stacks Project. The foundational 600+ page reference covering étale sites, proper base change, comparison theorems with singular cohomology, Poincaré duality, cycle classes, and the Grothendieck–Lefschetz trace formula.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/Lin_LefschetzFixedPointTheorem.pdf, Elementary Proof of the Lefschetz Fixed Point Theorem. Exposition linking the Lefschetz number $L(f) = \sum (-1)^i \operatorname{Tr}(f_* | H_i)$ to mapping degrees and Euler characteristics.

https://web.math.princeton.edu/~huh/ICM2022.pdf, Lorentzian Polynomials and Matroids. June Huh's ICM plenary address on Lorentzian polynomials, log-concavity, Hodge theory for matroids, and generalized permutohedra.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/articles/2005aX.pdf, Hermitian Symmetric Spaces and Shimura Varieties. J.S. Milne's classification of Riemannian and Hermitian symmetric domains and arithmetic quotients.

https://www.math.purdue.edu/~arapura/preprints/diffforms.pdf, Notes on Differential Forms. Donu Arapura's 53-page introduction to differential forms, exterior derivatives, Stokes' theorem, and de Rham cohomology.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0903.0517, Spreading Out and the Ax-Grothendieck Theorem. Serre's note on the Ax–Grothendieck theorem: any injective polynomial endomorphism of $\mathbb{C}^n$ is surjective by reduction to finite fields $\mathbb{F}_p$ via Nullstellensatz techniques.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1805.07172, Mod-2 Cohomological Invariants of Weyl Groups. Serre's computation of the ring of mod-2 cohomological invariants for symmetric groups and Weyl groups.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1708.00046, Mod-p Reduction of Orthogonal Representations. Serre's paper showing that the mod-$p$ reduction of characteristic-zero orthogonal and symplectic representations remains orthogonal/symplectic for $p > 2$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0305257, Subgroups of SO(3) and Platonic Symmetries. Serre's paper on finite subgroup embeddings $A_4 \cong \operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_3), S_4 \cong \operatorname{PGL}_2(\mathbb{F}_3), A_5 \cong \operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_5)$ in $\mathrm{SO}_3(\mathbb{R})$ and their applications.

https://wiki.epfl.ch/gant/documents/lecture2-cib2011.pdf, The Geometry of Adeles and Lattices. Adelic interpretation of modular forms as functions on $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Q}) \backslash \mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{A})$. Explains the transitiveness of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{Q})$ on lattices in $\mathbb{Q}_p^n$ with stabilizer $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{Z}_p)$.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/00I8, Valuation Rings in Scheme Theory. Definition of valuation rings via domination of local rings and characterization of directed colimits of valuation rings.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0605429, Algebraic K-Theory over the Field with One Element. Deitmar's paper computing the $K$-theory of $\mathbb{F}_1$ via Quillen's plus construction $B\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{F}_1)^+$, identifying $K_j(\mathbb{F}_1)$ with the stable homotopy groups of spheres $\pi_j^s(\mathbb{S})$.

https://www.ams.org/journals/tran/1991-327-02/S0002-9947-1991-1041048-5/S0002-9947-1991-1041048-5.pdf, Mellin Transforms and p-Adic Distributions. $\mathbb{Z}_p$-valued measures, finitely additive distributions on compact opens, and $p$-adic Mellin transforms.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1703.07842, The Artin Map via Selmer K-Theory. Dustin Clausen's paper interpreting the Artin reciprocity map as a natural transformation from algebraic $K$-homology to topological $K$-homology for fields with virtual cohomological dimension $\operatorname{vcd}(F) \le 2$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2506.18174, Dualities for Compact p-Adic Lie Groups. Clausen's cleaned-up thesis on Lazard–Poincaré duality and linearizations of $p$-adic Lie groups.

https://wstein.org/edu/2010/582e/lectures/all/galois_cohomology.pdf, Short Primer on Galois Cohomology. William Stein's concise notes covering $H^0, H^1, H^2$, Hilbert's Theorem 90, and Brauer groups.

https://www.mn.uio.no/math/personer/vit/rognes/papers/higher_arithmetic_duality.pdf, Higher Arithmetic Duality. John Rognes on Poincaré duality, perfect cup product pairings $H^k \otimes H^{n-k} \to k$, and trace/evaluation maps in arithmetic topology.

https://www.homepages.ucl.ac.uk/~ucaheps/topics/ODP_without_pauses.pdf, The Ordinary Double Point and Monodromy. Richard Thomas's visual notes on node singularities, vanishing cycles, and Picard–Lefschetz monodromy.

https://www.homepages.ucl.ac.uk/~ucaheps/topics/Chern_Classes_25.pdf, Vector Bundles and Chern Classes. Richard Thomas's intuitive introduction to vector bundles, transition functions, clutching maps, the Möbius strip, and Chern classes $c_1(L) \in H^2(X, \mathbb{Z})$.

https://www.ams.org/journals/bull/2001-38-03/S0273-0979-01-00909-0/S0273-0979-01-00909-0.pdf?t=1774525686525, History of the Finite Simple Group Classification. Solomon's historical survey of finite simple groups from Galois and Jordan to Sylow and the CFSG theorem.

https://math.stanford.edu/%7Econrad/249BW09Page/handouts/geomcft.pdf, Geometric Class Field Theory. Brian Conrad's handout on geometric class field theory for curves over finite fields using the Lang isogeny on Jacobians $\operatorname{Jac}(C)$.

https://lesesvre.perso.math.cnrs.fr/2021-tate_lecture-slides.pdf, Motivation for the Adeles and Tate's Thesis. Comprehensive slides on restricted direct products, local Haar measures, and Tate's thesis.

https://math.bu.edu/people/jsweinst/Math274/ScholzeLectures.pdf, Scholze's Lectures on Shtukas. Jared Weinstein's notes on Peter Scholze's lectures defining moduli spaces of shtukas and local Langlands for function fields.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1404.2156, Galois Theory in Homotopy Theory. Mathew's paper on Galois extensions of commutative ring spectra, Hopf–Galois theory, and descent in stable homotopy theory.

https://www.math.purdue.edu/~arapura/preprints/etale.pdf, Introduction to Étale Cohomology. Donu Arapura's 60-page introduction covering schemes as functors of points, Gelfand duality, the failure of Zariski topology for local inversions, and étale sites.

https://www.math.purdue.edu/~arapura/preprints/abelian.pdf, Abelian Varieties and Moduli. Arapura's 46-page primer on complex abelian varieties, line bundles, polarizations, and Siegel moduli spaces.

https://math.sustech.edu.cn/uploads/20200614/a92a231137226a567de67d8660fd0771.pdf, Fractional Calculus and Integral Operators. 86 slides on fractional derivatives, Abel's tautochrone problem, and Riemann–Liouville fractional integrals.

https://mathweb.ucsd.edu/~xatian/files/slides_ICERM.pdf, Nonlocal Differential Operators. Slides on nonlocal Laplacians $Lu(x) = \int K(|y-x|)(u(y)-u(x))\,dy$, Schur complements, and fractional diffusion.

https://seminari-simba.github.io/slides/simba23032018-SergiBaena.pdf, Fractional Laplace Operators. 115 slides on fractional Laplacian powers $(-\Delta)^s$, Lévy processes, and Kato's square root problem.

https://pabloraulstinga.github.io/Stinga%20-%20PhD%20Thesis.pdf, Fractional Powers of Second-Order PDEs. Stinga's thesis on extension techniques and Dirichlet-to-Neumann maps for fractional PDEs.

https://www.math.purdue.edu/~arapura/preprints/delgab.pdf, Purity and l-Adic Sheaves after Deligne-Gabber. Arapura's 11-page notes on étale Grothendieck topologies, constructible $\ell$-adic sheaves, and Gabber's purity theorem.

https://www.math.purdue.edu/~arapura/algebra/homological.pdf, Introduction to Homological Algebra. 70-page text covering projective and injective modules, internal Hom, tensor-hom adjunctions, derived functors, and the Quillen–Suslin theorem.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2107.02090, Horocycle Flows and Ergodic Integrals. Expository survey on asymptotic horocycle ergodic integrals on $\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbb{R})/\Gamma$, Ratner's theorem, and polynomial decay of correlations.

https://publications.ias.edu/sites/default/files/Mahler%20Colloquium%20%20Lecture%201-%20Horocycle%20Flows%20at%20Prime%20Times.pdf, Horocycle Flows at Prime Times. Peter Sarnak's Mahler Lectures on unipotent flows, nilmanifolds, Möbius randomness, and circle methods.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0712.1300, Introduction to Ratner's Equidistribution Theorem. Survey on unipotent group actions on homogeneous spaces and Ratner's measure classification theorem.

https://www.math.purdue.edu/~arapura/algebra/homological3.pdf, Ext Groups and Extensions. Notes on extensions of modules $0 \to A \to B \to C \to 0$, equivalence of extensions, Baer sums, and their classification via $\operatorname{Ext}^1(C, A)$.

https://irma.math.unistra.fr/~liu/OFM.pdf, Fourier-Mukai Transforms on Complex Tori. Derived push-pull transforms $\mathcal{F} \mapsto \mathbf{R}p_*(\mathcal{P} \otimes \mathbf{L}q^*\mathcal{F})$ on complex abelian varieties and principal bundles.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1701.00805, Borel Resummation in Physics. 9-page guide to asymptotic series, Borel transforms, and non-perturbative corrections in quantum field theory.

https://www.math.uni-bonn.de/~schwede/SymSpec-v3.pdf, Symmetric Spectra Book. Stefan Schwede's comprehensive monograph on diagram spectra, symmetric spectra, and stable model categories.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1709.00669, Homotopic Renormalization Group. Formulating the Wilsonian renormalization group flow within homotopical and $\infty$-categorical frameworks.

https://websites.umich.edu/~pixton/papers/tautological.pdf, The Tautological Ring of Moduli of Curves. Aaron Pixton's PhD thesis formulating relations in the tautological ring $R^*(\mathcal{M}_{g,n})$ using 3-spin Witten classes.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~lurie/252xnotes/Lecture2.pdf, Lazard's Theorem on Formal Groups. Jacob Lurie's notes on the universal 1-dimensional formal group law and the Lazard ring $L \cong \mathbb{Z}[x_1, x_2, \dots]$.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~lurie/papers/Conceptual.pdf, Conceptual Completeness by Jacob Lurie. Makkai's conceptual completeness theorem relating small pretopoi to ultra-functors on their categories of models, Stone duality, and ultrafilters.

https://golem.ph.utexas.edu/category/2011/07/definitions_of_ultrafilter.html, Ultrafilters from Multiple Perspectives. Tom Leinster's blog post exploring ultrafilters as {0,1}-valued finitely additive measures, partition detectors, and points of the Stone–Čech compactification $\beta X$.

https://aareyanmanzoor.github.io/assets/matsumura-CA.pdf, Matsumura's Commutative Algebra. Foundational text covering dimension theory, Cohen–Macaulay rings, regular local rings, and flat morphisms.

https://www.mathunion.org/fileadmin/ICM/Proceedings/ICM1986.1/ICM1986.1.ocr.pdf, Quantum Groups by V.G. Drinfeld. Drinfeld's ICM 1986 address defining quantum groups, Hopf algebras, quasi-triangular structures, and the quantum Yang–Baxter equation.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/xnotes/tc.pdf, Notes on Tannakian Categories. J.S. Milne's comprehensive lecture notes on rigid symmetric monoidal categories and fiber functors.

https://www.ams.org/journals/bull/2001-38-04/S0273-0979-01-00913-2/S0273-0979-01-00913-2.pdf, Pierre Cartier on Grothendieck. Survey of Alexander Grothendieck's vision of topos theory, schemes, motives, and the ontological shift from points to spaces.

https://www.sas.rochester.edu/mth/sites/doug-ravenel/mybooks/esht-new.pdf, The Kervaire Invariant Problem. Hill, Hopkins, and Ravenel's book solving the Kervaire invariant one problem in all dimensions except 126 via equivariant stable homotopy theory.

https://www.sas.rochester.edu/mth/sites/doug-ravenel/mybooks/ravenel.pdf, Complex Cobordism and Stable Homotopy Groups. Douglas Ravenel's classic "Green Book" on the Adams–Novikov spectral sequence and chromatic homotopy theory.

https://www.sas.rochester.edu/mth/sites/doug-ravenel/mybooks/nilpb2020.pdf, Nilpotence and Periodicity. Ravenel's book on the Devinatz–Hopkins–Smith nilpotence and periodicity theorems in stable homotopy theory.

https://web.math.ku.dk/~moller/e01/algtopI/comments.pdf, Comments on Hatcher's Algebraic Topology. Notes on Chapter 0 of Hatcher covering cell complexes, homotopy extension properties, and mapping cones.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/gaitsgde/Book/HA.pdf, Notes on Higher Algebra by Dennis Gaitsgory. Foundational text on stable $\infty$-categories, dualizable categories, ind-completions, Verdier duality on $\mathcal{D}\text{-}\mathbf{Mod}(X)$, and commutative differential graded algebras.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/Fractals1Dimension2026.pdf, Notions of Dimension in Physics and Geometry. Matilde Marcolli's slides on fractal dimensions, dimensional regularization in QFT, and differential forms.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/ManinLogicforMathematicians.pdf, A Course in Mathematical Logic. Yuri Manin's text on mathematical logic, computability, model theory, and constructive mathematics.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/MoerdijkOosten.pdf, Sets, Models and Proofs. Moerdijk and van Oosten's notes on intuitionistic logic, model theory, and topoi.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/Ma6PropositionalLogic.pdf, Propositional Logic Slides. Lecture slides on Boolean algebras, Heyting algebras, and classical vs intuitionistic logic.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/ComplexitiesSlides2021Ma148.pdf, Kolmogorov and Effective Complexity. Marcolli's slides on algorithmic information theory, Kraft's inequality, Levin's universal distribution, Shannon entropy, and logical depth.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/ClassicalQInfoSlidesMa148aFall2021.pdf, Classical and Quantum Information. Slides on quantum entropy, density matrices, and quantum channels.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/InformationAlgebras2021Ma148.pdf, Information Algebras and Thermodynamic Semirings. Algebraic structures representing information loss, Tsallis entropy, and max-plus algebras.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/CodesComplexitySlides2021Ma148.pdf, Error-Correcting Codes and Complexity. Linear codes, Hamming bounds, and computational complexity theory.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/SlidesCRSS2021Ma148.pdf, Classical to Quantum Error-Correcting Codes. Transition from classical linear codes to CSS and stabilizer quantum error-correcting codes.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/GammaSpaceSlides2021Ma148.pdf, Gamma-Spaces and Information. Segal's $\Gamma$-spaces, information categories, and algebraic $K$-theory.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/RenormalizationComputationSlides2021Ma148.pdf, Renormalization, Computation and Anyons. Connes–Kreimer Hopf algebras of Feynman graphs, anyon systems, and topological quantum computation.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/EntropySlides2021Ma148.pdf, Entropy and Geometry over the Field with One Element. Detailed slides on Shannon and Tsallis entropies, Khinchin axioms, escort distributions, and statistical manifolds. Connects entropy chain rules to Hochschild cohomology, $F_1$-geometry, and finite polylogarithms $\sum_{k=1}^{p-1} x^k/k^n \pmod p$.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/ConvexityDensitiesSlides2021Ma148.pdf, Convexity, Simplicial Sets, and Probability. Convex bodies, pure states, the Hahn–Banach separation theorem, simplicial sets as local mixing models, cubical sets, and the category of finite probability spaces.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/gaitsgde/GL/Ran.pdf, The Ran Space and Factorization Sheaves. Gaitsgory's notes on the Ran space $\operatorname{Ran}(X)$, chiral algebras, and factorization sheaves in the geometric Langlands program.

http://mattbooth.info/notes/hodgeproject.pdf, Commutative Differential Graded Algebras. Introduction to CDGAs, Sullivan minimal models, and rational homotopy theory.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/gaitsgde/Book/BasicsDAG.pdf, Basics of Derived Algebraic Geometry. Gaitsgory and Rozenblyum's notes on prestacks, derived affine schemes $\mathbf{Aff}_{\text{der}} \cong \mathbf{CDGA}^{\le 0}$, convergence, flat/étale descent, and derived Artin stacks.

https://webusers.imj-prg.fr/~pierre.schapira/ConfSurveys/GrTopAn.pdf, Grothendieck Topologies for Analysts. Pierre Schapira's survey on subanalytic sites, tempered holomorphic functions, constructible sheaves, and Sato's hyperfunctions without functional analytic pathologies.

https://webusers.imj-prg.fr/~pierre.schapira/LectNotes/MuShv.pdf, Microlocal Theory of Sheaves. Kashiwara and Schapira's microlocal sheaf theory, micro-supports $\operatorname{SS}(\mathcal{F}) \subset T^*M$, and involutive geometry on cotangent bundles.

https://math.mit.edu/~rbm/18.157Sp22/18.157Sp22+.pdf, Lectures on Microlocal Analysis. Richard Melrose's 170-page course notes on pseudo-differential operators, wave front sets, and propagation of singularities.

https://pbelmans.ncag.info/notes/etale-cohomology.pdf, Comprehensive Étale Cohomology Notes. Pieter Belmans' notes detailing Grothendieck topologies (Zariski, étale, fppf, fpqc), constructible sheaves, proper base change, Gysin maps, and the Weil cohomology axioms.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~willfisher/papers/Lie_Algebra_Cohomology.pdf, Lie Algebra Cohomology in 3 Pages. Chevalley–Eilenberg complex $\operatorname{CE}(\mathfrak{g}) = (\bigwedge^* \mathfrak{g}^*, d)$, where the differential $d$ is the dual of the Lie bracket. Proves $H^*(\operatorname{CE}(\mathfrak{g})) \cong H_{\text{dR}}^*(G)$ for compact connected Lie groups.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~jxwang/GaugeTheoryVirtualSlides.pdf, Gauge Theory and Cosmetic Crossings. Slides on cosmetic crossing conjectures in knot theory via instanton and monopole Floer homology.

https://math.uchicago.edu/~may/REU2022/REUPapers/Fang,%20Xingzhu.pdf, The Geometric Satake Equivalence. 26-page undergraduate REU paper explaining the Geometric Satake correspondence $\operatorname{Perv}_{G(\mathcal{O})}(\operatorname{Gr}_G) \cong \operatorname{Rep}(G^\vee)$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1703.07288, Survey on the Geometric Satake Equivalence. 115-page comprehensive survey on the affine Grassmannian, convolution Grassmannians, Mirković–Vilonen cycles, and Tannakian reconstruction.

https://makoba.gitlab.io/ws2021-algebraic-groups-actions/group-actions.pdf, Actions of Algebraic Groups. 3-page summary of algebraic group actions, orbits, and stabilizers.

https://max-mornev.github.io/et/BrauerGroups.pdf, Summary on Brauer Groups. Notes covering Azumaya algebras, central simple algebras, étale cohomology $H^2_{\text{ét}}(X, \mathbb{G}_m)$, and the Brauer–Manin obstruction.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~zyao/notes/notes/LLC.pdf, The Local Langlands Correspondence for GL(n). Z. Yao's notes on the classification of irreducible admissible representations of $\mathrm{GL}_n(F)$ and local Langlands.

https://www.keerthimadapusi.com/assets/talks/2025-02-19-darmstadt.pdf, Prismatic F-Gauges and Display Theory. Keerthi Madapusi's slides on $F$-gauges, displays, and $p$-divisible groups.

http://www.tac.mta.ca/tac/volumes/11/4/11-04.pdf, The Projection Formula in Abstract Homotopy Theory. Fausk, Hu, and May on the projection formula, Wirthmüller contexts, and Grothendieck duality contexts in closed symmetric monoidal categories.

https://www.keerthimadapusi.com/assets/old_notes/logpdiv.pdf, Logarithmic p-Divisible Groups. Notes on log structures, log $p$-divisible groups, and degenerations of abelian varieties.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/index.html, Harmonic Analysis Course. Po-Lam Yung's lecture materials covering real-variable harmonic analysis.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/Lecture1.pdf, The Fourier Transform on Euclidean Space. Properties of the Fourier transform on $L^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$ and the Schwartz space $\mathcal{S}(\mathbb{R}^n)$.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/Lecture2.pdf, Tempered Distributions and Harmonic Functions. Duality between $\mathcal{S}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ and $\mathcal{S}'(\mathbb{R}^n)$, Poisson integrals, and harmonic extensions.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/Lecture3.pdf, Maximal Functions and Riesz Potentials. Hardy–Littlewood maximal operators, fractional integrals, and the Hardy–Littlewood–Sobolev inequality.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/Lecture4.pdf, Singular Integrals and Littlewood-Paley Decompositions. Calderón–Zygmund singular integrals, dyadic partitions of unity, and Littlewood–Paley square functions.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/Lecture5.pdf, Function Spaces: Holder, Sobolev, and BMO. Characterizations of Hölder spaces $C^\alpha$, Sobolev spaces $W^{k,p}$, and John–Nirenberg BMO spaces.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/Lecture6.pdf, Pseudodifferential Calculus. Symbols, amplitudes, and $L^2$-boundedness of pseudo-differential operators.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/Lecture7.pdf, Paraproducts and the T(1) Theorem. Coifman–Meyer paraproducts, Carleson measures, and the David–Journé $T(1)$ theorem.

https://maths-people.anu.edu.au/~plyung/math6081/Lecture8.pdf, Interpolation of Linear Operators. Riesz–Thorin and Marcinkiewicz interpolation theorems and endpoint bounds.

https://mathoverflow.net/questions/67228/where-do-all-these-projection-formulas-come-from, Origin of the Projection Formula. Discussion tracing the projection formula $f_*(A \otimes f^*B) \cong f_*A \otimes B$ to Frobenius reciprocity in representation theory and derived adjunctions.

https://static.ias.edu/pitp/2018/sites/pitp/files/witten_lecture_2.pdf, Entanglement and Operator Algebras in QFT. Edward Witten's lectures on entanglement entropy, Type III factors, and Tomita–Takesaki modular theory in quantum field theory.

https://www.maths.ox.ac.uk/system/files/media/HarmAnSlides.pdf, Oxford Harmonic Analysis Course. 80 slides covering Pontryagin duality, Haar measures, translation-invariant operators, convolutions, approximations of the identity (Poisson, Gaussian, Fejér kernels), distribution theory, and Poisson summation formulas.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.19524, Covering Spaces and Sites in Galois Theory. Formulating classical Galois theory and covering spaces through Grothendieck topologies and étale sites.

https://users.monash.edu/~normd/documents/Completion-Talk.pdf, Intersection Theory of Moduli Spaces. 31 slides on boundary divisors, compactifications, and intersection numbers on $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}$.

https://swc-math.github.io/aws/2025/2025ChanOutline.pdf, Outline of p-Adic Deligne-Lusztig Theory. 3-page outline connecting representations of $G(\mathbb{F}_q)$ to $\ell$-adic étale cohomology of Deligne–Lusztig varieties.

https://www.math.uchicago.edu/~bloch/albert_lects/cycletalk1.pdf, Spencer Bloch on Algebraic Cycles. Lectures on algebraic cycles, Chow groups $\operatorname{CH}^i(X)$, motivic cohomology, and their relations to mixed Hodge structures and Beilinson's conjectures on $L$-values.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/UPL6498344618891550944_AN_93_connes.pdf, Noncommutative Geometry and Compact Operators. Alain Connes on compact operators in Hilbert space acting as infinitesimals, Dixmier traces, and non-commutative integration.

https://cisinski.app.uni-regensburg.de/traces_LNM.pdf, Cohomological Methods in Intersection Theory. Denis-Charles Cisinski's 56-page notes proving Grothendieck–Verdier duality for étale motives and motivic base change.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~tao/preprints/compactness.pdf, Why Compactness Matters. Terence Tao's 4-page essay on compactness as a local-to-global principle, extraction of convergent subsequences, and compactifications (Alexandroff vs Stone–Čech).

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/9906200, Ind-Sheaves and Distribution Theory. Kashiwara and Schapira's paper developing the category of ind-sheaves to provide a purely algebraic and sheaf-theoretic foundation for distributions and hyperfunctions.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1807.04274, Gluing in Quantum Field Theory I. Formulating cutting and gluing laws for path integrals and boundary conditions in topological quantum field theories.

https://www.math.purdue.edu/~arapura/preprints/partI.pdf, Basic Open Sets and Localization. Donu Arapura's notes explaining how ring localizations $R[1/g]$ form the basic distinguished open sets $D(g)$ of the Zariski topology.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/IntroNCGCaltech1.pdf, Differential Geometry Primer for NCG. Matilde Marcolli's notes reviewing smooth manifolds, transition functions, connections, Riemannian metrics, curvature 2-forms, Yang–Mills gauge theories, and action functionals.

Marcolli's notes covering noncommutative spaces via groupoid algebras $A(X/R) = C^*(\mathcal{G}_R)$, the Serre–Swan theorem, Spin geometry, Clifford algebras, Dirac operators, and spectral triples $(\mathcal{A}, \mathcal{H}, \mathcal{D})$.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/IntroNCGCaltech3.pdf, Finite Spectral Triples and the Standard Model. Classification of finite-dimensional spectral triples, Kasparov $KK$-products, and the metric structure on discrete noncommutative spaces.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1209.3606, The Codensity Monad of a Functor. Tom Leinster's paper proving that the codensity monad of the inclusion $\mathbf{FinSet} \hookrightarrow \mathbf{Set}$ is the ultrafilter monad, connecting Kan extensions to integration against ultrafilters.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.01863, Chromatic Redshift and Counterexamples. Studies algebraic $K$-theory of ring spectra, the redshift conjecture, and rigid stability.

https://www.keerthimadapusi.com/assets/old_notes/NOS.pdf, Notes on Sheaf Theory. Keerthi Madapusi's 26-page primer on presheaves, sheafification, stalks, and direct/inverse images.

https://math.stanford.edu/~conrad/papers/hypercover.pdf, Cohomological Descent and Hypercovers. Brian Conrad's notes on simplicial techniques, hypercoverings, and Čech-to-derived spectral sequences.

https://imsarchives.nus.edu.sg/oldwww2/events/2018/lang/files/nicolas.pdf, Ramanujan Conjecture for Function Fields. Overview of Laurent Lafforgue's proof of the Langlands correspondence and Ramanujan conjecture for $\mathrm{GL}_n$ over function fields.

https://sites.math.duke.edu/~dasgupta/papers/SH.pdf, Foundations of Class Field Theory. Samit Dasgupta's 36 slides covering the Kronecker–Weber theorem, Artin reciprocity, ray class fields, and $L$-functions.

https://www.ms.u-tokyo.ac.jp/~t-saito/pp/GR2.pdf, Ramification Theory of Schemes. Takeshi Saito's survey on wild ramification, conductor formulas, and the étale cohomology of Fermat curves.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~hhao/notes/Adelic%20Line%20Bundles%20and%20Bogomolov%20Conjecture.pdf, Adelic Line Bundles and the Bogomolov Conjecture. 15-page notes on arithmetic heights, adelic line bundles, and the Bogomolov conjecture on curves.

https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/18-786-number-theory-ii-class-field-theory-spring-2016/b594a0782d952277f82601576981c775_MIT18_786S16_lec7.pdf, Herbrand Quotients and Unit Groups. MIT OpenCourseWare notes on Herbrand quotients $h(M) = |H^2(G, M)| / |H^1(G, M)|$ and their vanishing on induced modules.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/download/coherent.pdf#nameddest=01XS, Cohomology of Projective Space. Stacks project tag 01XS computing the cohomology groups $H^q(\mathbb{P}^n, \mathcal{O}(d))$.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~jas/writings/cft-inequalities.pdf, Fundamental Inequalities in Class Field Theory. Detailed proofs of the first and second fundamental inequalities of class field theory using Herbrand quotients.

https://people.reed.edu/~jerry/311/ecintro.pdf, Introduction to Elliptic Curves. Jerry Shurman's 13-page introductory notes on Weierstrass equations, the chord-and-tangent group law, and complex lattices $\mathbb{C}/\Lambda$.

https://1429cecd-24a0-4223-8b7c-1ebf47aa12e2.filesusr.com/ugd/8e912a_fe1c2f8e90094d0fa065fb8129687963.pdf, Fractured Structures on Condensed Anima. 61-page monograph on fractured $\infty$-topoi and condensed higher homotopy types.

https://www.math.uni-bonn.de/~schwede/equivariant.pdf, Equivariant Homotopy Theory. Stefan Schwede's 113-page lecture notes on genuine equivariant spectra, Mackey functors, and orthogonal $G$-spectra.

https://home.hiroshima-u.ac.jp/ichiro-shimada/slides/2022AugNUS/2022AugTalkNUSShimada.pdf, Mordell-Weil Groups and Niemeier Lattices. Slides on elliptic $K3$ surfaces, Mordell–Weil lattices, and the 24 positive-definite even unimodular Niemeier lattices in dimension 24.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2111.00069, A Short Proof of Straightening and Unstraightening. Direct proof of Lurie's equivalence between Cartesian fibrations $p: \mathcal{X} \to \mathcal{S}$ and functors $\mathcal{S}^{\text{op}} \to \mathbf{Cat}_\infty$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2111.10203, Chromatic Redshift in Higher Algebra. Christian Schlenk on chromatic redshift in algebraic $K$-theory and higher semiadditive $\infty$-categories.

https://www.math.columbia.edu/~chaoli/tutorial2012/knots-and-primes.pdf, Knots and Primes: Arithmetic Topology. Chao Li's notes on the Kapranov–Reznikov–Mazur dictionary linking knots in 3-manifolds to primes in $\operatorname{Spec} \mathbb{Z}$, linking numbers to the Legendre symbol, and Alexander polynomials to Iwasawa polynomials.

https://people.kth.se/~akarl/langmemorial.pdf, Quadratic Reciprocity via Theta Functions. Anders Karlsson's proof of Gauss's quadratic reciprocity law using the Poisson summation formula for Jacobi theta functions.

https://people.reed.edu/~jerry/361/lectures/feqn.pdf, Functional Equations for Dirichlet L-Functions. Normalization, completed $L$-functions $\Lambda(s, \chi)$, and functional equations via theta inversions.

https://hott.github.io/book/hott-online-78-g0bb33d2.pdf, Homotopy Type Theory Book. The Univalent Foundations Program's book on synthetic homotopy theory, identity types, the Univalence Axiom, and higher inductive types.

https://home.sandiego.edu/~shulman/papers/stthits.pdf, Higher Inductive Types in Simplicial Type Theory. Mike Shulman's slides on the semantics of $W$-types and higher inductive types in homotopical models.

https://math.stanford.edu/~vakil/725/class21.pdf, Line Bundles and Divisors by Ravi Vakil. Total spaces of line bundles, invertible sheaves of sections, Cartier divisors, and the non-triviality of the tautological line bundle $\mathcal{O}(-1)$ on $\mathbb{P}^1$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0303175, Categorical Descent Theory. Ross Street's survey on 2-categorical descent, fibered categories, and higher stacks.

https://www.kwlan.org/articles/cpt-PEL-type-thesis.pdf, Compactifications of PEL-Type Shimura Varieties. Kai-Wen Lan's thesis establishing toroidal and minimal compactifications of PEL-type Shimura varieties.

https://jsempliner.github.io/shim2.pdf, Formalism of Shimura Varieties. 83-page notes on Shimura data, the conjugation of Shimura varieties, and canonical models.

https://www.math.columbia.edu/~harris/resarticles/Yalecolloquium.pdf, The Langlands Program and Geometry. Michael Harris's colloquium slides outlining how automorphic forms organize Galois representations through the cohomology of Shimura varieties.

https://www.math.mcgill.ca/darmon/courses/20-21/cm/francesc-notes.pdf, Complex Multiplication of Elliptic Curves. 116-page lecture notes on the theory of complex multiplication, class field generation, and Shimura–Taniyama formulas.

https://ncatlab.org/davidcorfield/files/Vienna4.pdf, Philosophy of Higher Category Theory. David Corfield's paper on the structuralist evolution of mathematical language from sets to $\infty$-topoi.

https://www.kurims.kyoto-u.ac.jp/~kyodo/kokyuroku/contents/pdf/1451-16.pdf, Bloch-Kato Reciprocity and Higher Chow Groups. Formulating higher reciprocity maps from the 0-th Chow group of 0-cycles to the étale fundamental group.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/DelaneyModularTensorCategories.pdf, Modular Tensor Categories. Lecture notes on ribbon categories, modular tensor categories, and link invariants.

https://serggasp.github.io/comp6741/glossary.pdf, Graph Theory Glossary. Concise definitions of directed acyclic graphs, bipartite graphs, independent sets, and Boolean formulas.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/Rezk_ModelCategoryForCategories.pdf, Model Categories for Categories. Charles Rezk's notes on model structures on $\mathbf{Cat}$, equivalences, and isofibrations.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/StableChromaticGlossary.pdf, Stable Chromatic Glossary. Rapid reference defining $A_\infty$-rings, $E_\infty$-rings, symmetric spectra, orthogonal spectra, and chromatic localization.

http://wwwf.imperial.ac.uk/~buzzard/xena/pdfs/liquid_tensor_experiment.pdf, The Liquid Tensor Experiment. Kevin Buzzard's report on formalizing the Clausen–Scholze theorem in Lean: the vanishing of $\operatorname{Ext}^i(\mathcal{M}_{p'}(S), V) = 0$ for $p$-liquid Banach spaces $V$ and $p'$-measures. Covers condensed sets, Stone–Čech compactifications of profinite sets, solid abelian groups, and liquid vector spaces.

https://people.math.binghamton.edu/malkiewich/stable.pdf, Summary of Stable Homotopy Theory. Cary Malkiewich's 9-page guide defining spectra, stable equivalences, smash products, and sphere spectra.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/gaitsgde/grad_2009/SeminarNotes/Nov17-19(Crystals).pdf, Crystals and D-Modules. Dennis Gaitsgory's notes on algebraic vector bundles with flat connections and crystal categories on schemes.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/gaitsgde/GL/Stackstext.pdf, Short Notes on Stacks. Gaitsgory's notes on prestacks as functors $\mathbf{CDGA}^{\le 0} \to \operatorname{Grpd}_\infty$ and $n$-connective truncations.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/LurieChromaticHomotopyTheory.pdf, Jacob Lurie's Chromatic Homotopy Theory. 300+ page lecture notes covering formal group laws, the Landweber exact functor theorem, height filtrations, Morava $K$-theories, and the chromatic spectral sequence.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/scholze/Complex.pdf, Condensed Complex Geometry. Dustin Clausen and Peter Scholze's 111-page masterclass notes. Develops condensed mathematics, liquid vector spaces $\operatorname{Liquid}_p$, analytic rings, dual nuclear Fréchet (DNF) spaces, and categorified locales over $\mathcal{D}(\operatorname{Liq}_p)$ to prove a non-archimedean and complex analytic GAGA theorem.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~fengt/282C.pdf, The Atiyah-Singer Index Theorem and Physics. Tony Feng's course notes on topological $K$-theory, heat kernel asymptotics, Dirac operators, and supersymmetric quantum mechanics.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2408.07693, Elliptic Cohomology and Physics. Comprehensive survey on topological modular forms ($\operatorname{tmf}$), 2D supersymmetric field theories, and Borcherds vertex operator algebras.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1001.2282, Noncommutative Motives. Blumberg, Gepner, and Tabuada on the universal localizing invariant $U_{\text{loc}}: \mathbf{Cat}_\infty^{\text{perf}} \to \operatorname{Mot}_{\text{nc}}$ and its relation to algebraic $K$-theory, cyclic homology, and topological Hochschild homology.

https://math.mit.edu/juvitop/old/notes/2015_Fall/NotesF6.pdf, Beilinson and Bloch-Kato Conjectures. Lecture notes connecting algebraic $K$-theory, higher regulators, and special values of $L$-functions (including the BSD conjecture).

https://www.arxiv.org/pdf/hep-th/9907140v1, D-Branes and Topological K-Theory. Edward Witten on classifying D-brane charges and Ramond–Ramond fields in string theory via topological $K$-theory.

https://www.imo.universite-paris-saclay.fr/~jean-michel.bismut/Bismut/1991.pdf, Quillen Metrics and Complex Geometry. Jean-Michel Bismut's foundational paper on Quillen metrics on determinant lines, ray-Singer analytic torsion, and Arakelov geometry.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1601.06285, Algebraic K-Theory of Topological K-Theory. Studies $K(\operatorname{KU})$ and its role in T-duality and chromatic redshift.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/hep-th/0309108, Nonlinear Electrodynamics and D-Branes. Born–Infeld actions and nonlinear electromagnetic dualities in string theory.

https://math.uchicago.edu/~may/REU2015/REUPapers/Kirsche.pdf, Topological K-Theory and Bott Periodicity. REU paper detailing the proof of Bott periodicity $K(X \times S^2) \cong K(X) \otimes K(S^2)$ and its real counterpart $KO$.

https://math.uchicago.edu/~may/REU2016/REUPapers/Banerjee.pdf, The Hopf Invariant One Problem. Exposition of the Adams–Atiyah solution to the Hopf invariant one problem using Adams operations in topological $K$-theory.

http://van-der-geer.nl/~gerard/ff.pdf, Curves over Finite Fields. Gerard van der Geer's survey on point counting on algebraic curves over $\mathbb{F}_q$ and the Weil bounds.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/scholze/DeformationSpaces.pdf, Deformation Spaces of p-Divisible Groups. Scholze and Weinstein's monograph on Lubin–Tate and Rapoport–Zink deformation spaces as adic spaces.

https://www.math.uni-bonn.de/people/schwein/documents/rutgers21.pdf, The Weil Group in the Langlands Program. Schwein's slides explaining how the Weil group $W_K$ and the Weil–Deligne group replace the absolute Galois group in local Langlands reciprocity.

https://indico.math.cnrs.fr/category/603/attachments/3705/9114/Expose%CC%81-ATR_IHP-Pierre-2025-v2.pdf, Complex Cubic Fields and Elliptic Gamma Functions. Pierre Charollois's slides on constructing Stark units in complex cubic number fields as special values of elliptic gamma functions.

https://hal.science/hal-04273703/document, Stark Units for Complex Cubic Fields. Explicit construction of algebraic units in complex cubic extensions via elliptic gamma special values.

https://kconrad.math.uconn.edu/blurbs/gradnumthy/unittheorem.pdf, Dirichlet's Unit Theorem. Keith Conrad's blurb detailing the rank $r = r_1 + r_2 - 1$ of the unit group $\mathcal{O}_K^\times$ and explicit fundamental units for real quadratic, complex cubic, and totally imaginary quartic fields.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~teleman/math/simpson.pdf, De Rham Cohomology of Stacks. Constantin Teleman's notes on Simpson's non-abelian Hodge theory, de Rham cohomology of stacks, and Hodge-to-de Rham spectral sequences.

https://smirnov.web.unc.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/22032/2022/08/OkounkovSmirnov_QuantumDiffs.pdf, Quantum Differences and Nakajima Varieties. Okounkov and Smirnov on quantum dynamical Weyl group actions in the equivariant $K$-theory of Nakajima quiver varieties.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/zagier/files/cntp-3-2/fulltext.pdf, Partition Functions in Complex Chern-Simons Theory. Dimofte, Gukov, and Zagier on computing all-loop perturbative partition functions in complex $G_\mathbb{C}$ Chern–Simons theory.

https://ymsc.tsinghua.edu.cn/__local/0/AC/4D/E324A74E5E166B4093CD1439F29_95B0BAFA_7763F0.pdf?e=.pdf, Dan Freed on Chern-Simons Theory. Lecture slides on classical and quantum Chern–Simons gauge theories, path integrals, and finite-dimensional modular tensor categories.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.04494, The Derived Tate Curve. Construction of the derived Tate curve in equivariant stable homotopy theory and spectral algebraic geometry.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~lurie/papers/survey.pdf, Elliptic Cohomology and Derived Algebraic Geometry. Jacob Lurie's survey on formal groups, even periodic cohomology theories, complex orientations, and the moduli stack of elliptic curves.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0802.1006, Large-Scale Phenomena in the Homotopy Groups of Spheres. Mark Mahowald's five lectures on chromatic phenomena, the Adams–Novikov spectral sequence, and periodicity in stable homotopy groups.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2206.02192, Sup-Norm Problems for Siegel Modular Forms. Classical sup-norm bounds for holomorphic Jacobi and Siegel modular forms over arithmetic quotients.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2512.14854, Mathematical Structures in Scattering Amplitudes. Survey on modern mathematical methods in QFT amplitudes: Grassmannians, cluster algebras, and motivic polylogarithms.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1803.08585, Cluster Algebras and Motivic Correlators. Alexander Goncharov on the interplay between cluster varieties, motivic correlators, and scattering amplitudes.

https://web.math.princeton.edu/~gyujino/Sarnak_course.pdf, Spectral Theory of Automorphic Forms. Cohen and Sarnak's course notes explaining Selberg's and Bernstein's proofs of the meromorphic continuation of Eisenstein series.

https://w3.impa.br/~hossein/myarticles/ModularFormsEllipticCurves.pdf, Modular Forms and Differential Equations. Hossein Movasati's book studying modular forms and Calabi–Yau differential equations through Halphen and Ramanujan systems.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1706.10100, Holomorphic Anomaly Equations for Elliptic Curves. Generating series of Gromov–Witten classes of elliptic curves, cycle-valued quasi-modular forms, and holomorphic anomaly equations.

https://quantum-journal.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/q-2024-05-08-1340-1.pdf, Haar Measure Tools in Quantum Information. Accessible tutorial on Haar integration over unitary groups using basic linear algebra and Weingarten calculus.

https://publications.ias.edu/sites/default/files/maass-cusp-sarnak.pdf, Maass Cusp Forms with Eigenvalue 1/4. Peter Sarnak's short paper classifying Maass cusp forms with eigenvalue $\lambda = 1/4$ and integral Fourier coefficients via symmetric power functoriality.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2302.12062, Wild Quantum Dilogarithms and Quivers. Deriving five-term quantum dilogarithm identities from the representation theory of quivers and motivic Donaldson–Thomas wall-crossing formulas.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/stavros/publications/printed/quantum_knot_invariants.pdf, Quantum Knot Invariants and the Jones Polynomial. Stavros Garoufalidis on colored Jones polynomials, hyperbolic 3-manifold geometry, the volume conjecture, and modularity.

https://dec41.user.srcf.net/notes/III_L/advanced_quantum_field_theory_trim.pdf, Advanced Quantum Field Theory. Cambridge Part III lecture notes on functional path integrals, non-abelian gauge theories, BRST quantization, and the renormalization group.

https://www.nikhef.nl/~t58/CFTBW.pdf, Conformal Field Theory Notes. Comprehensive notes on 2D CFT, the Virasoro algebra, Kac determinant formulas, modular invariance, and minimal models.

https://bonndoc.ulb.uni-bonn.de/xmlui/bitstream/handle/20.500.11811/10125/6739.pdf?sequence=2&isAllowed=y, The Six-Functor Formalism on Small v-Stacks. PhD thesis applying condensed mathematics and Clausen–Scholze foundations to establish the full six-functor formalism for $p$-torsion étale sheaves on small $v$-stacks.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.16196, Motivic Homotopy and p-Adic Cohomology. Survey of motivic nearby cycles, monodromy operators, and the $p$-adic weight-monodromy conjecture.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0709.1970, B-Pairs and p-Adic Galois Representations. Laurent Berger's paper on $B$-pairs, trianguline representations, and Tate local duality in Galois cohomology.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2502.00478, Inhomogeneous Spin q-Whittaker Polynomials. Orthogonality of spin $q$-Whittaker polynomials with respect to Sklyanin measures on the $n$-torus and eigenrelations.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2306.08644, Higher-Genus Modular Tensors and Polylogarithms. Constructing explicit elliptic and higher-genus polylogarithms via convolutions of Arakelov Green functions with holomorphic abelian differentials.

https://publications.ias.edu/sites/default/files/theory-of-eisenstein-systems-review_rpl_3.pdf, On the Theory of Eisenstein Systems. Robert Langlands' foundational treatise on the general spectral decomposition and Eisenstein series on reductive groups.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1312.7394, Topological Modular Forms with Level Structure. Extending the spectrum of topological modular forms $\operatorname{Tmf}$ to modular curves with level structure and cusp restrictions.

https://legacy-www.math.harvard.edu/theses/senior/krulewski/krulewski.pdf, Topological Insulators and Superconductors. Senior thesis covering symmetry-protected topological phases, bulk-boundary correspondences, and $K$-theory classifications.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1202.2110, Beyond Endoscopy via the Trace Formula. Robert Langlands' proposal for proving functoriality using the Arthur–Selberg trace formula.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math-ph/9908029, Mathematical Foundations of Quantum Field Theory. Survey focusing on complexification, polarization, and Euclidean vs Minkowskian structures in field theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2401.02568, Stone Duality in Condensed Algebraic Geometry. Establishing Stone duality between condensed sets and fpqc-sheaves over fields via Bousfield–Kan localization.

https://math.rice.edu/~ar99/Neuchatel2.pdf, Hyperbolic 3-Manifolds and Knot Complements. Alan Reid's slides on Thurston's hyperbolization theorem, trace fields $\mathbb{Q}(\operatorname{tr} \Gamma)$, Dehn surgery, and quaternion algebras.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/zagier/files/preprints/HyperbolicAndNeumann.pdf, Walter Neumann's Work in Topology and Number Theory. Don Zagier's review of Neumann's contributions to 3-manifold triangulations, Bloch groups, and algebraic $K$-theory.

https://math.commelin.net/files/wdreps.pdf, Weil-Deligne Representations. Johan Commelin's notes defining Weil–Deligne representations and local Langlands parameters.

https://www.i-repository.net/contents/osakacu/sugaku/00306126-59-2-315.pdf, Topological Monodromies of Degenerating Riemann Surfaces. Topological monodromy assortments of degenerating families of curves and negative Dehn twists on singular fibers.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0807.4855, Hodge Correlators and Feynman Integrals. Alexander Goncharov showing that Feynman amplitudes on complex varieties define functorial real mixed Hodge structures and motivic periods.

https://annals.math.princeton.edu/wp-content/uploads/annals-v173-n3-p12-p.pdf, The Local Langlands Conjecture for GSp(4). Gan and Takeda's proof of the local Langlands conjecture for $\operatorname{GSp}_4(F)$ over non-archimedean local fields.

https://webusers.imj-prg.fr/~julien.marche/Ref_GL_GT/Bump.pdf, Introduction to the Langlands Program. Daniel Bump's introductory survey on automorphic forms, $L$-functions, and the Langlands conjectures.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0911.5328, DAHA and Affine Flag Manifolds. Geometric construction of double affine Hecke algebras (DAHA) via equivariant homology of affine flag varieties.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1802.07138, BRST-BV Quantization of Gauge Theories. Functional integral quantization of gauge theories with open and reducible gauge algebras using the Batalin–Vilkovisky formalism.

http://javier.fresan.perso.math.cnrs.fr/expmot.pdf, Exponential Motives. Javier Fresán and Peter Jossen's monograph constructing the Tannakian category of exponential motives over subfields of $\mathbb{C}$ with Betti and de Rham realizations.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0111175, Eta Invariants and Regularized Determinants. Juhl's paper on eta invariants of Dirac operators, regularized determinants on hyperbolic manifolds with cusps, and Selberg zeta functions.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1405.0356, Introduction to 1-Summability and Resurgence. Survey of 1-summability, formal Borel transforms, Laplace transforms, and Gevrey asymptotics.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1702.04148, Resurgence and Lefschetz Thimbles in Quantum Mechanics. Asymptotic series, Lefschetz thimbles, and Borel resummability of path integrals with discrete spectra.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/zagier/files/tex/ECMHirzebruchLecture/ECMHirzebruchLectureCorrected.pdf, Differential Equations in Number Theory and Geometry. Don Zagier's corrected Hirzebruch lecture on modular forms, periods, and topological differential equations.

https://math.uchicago.edu/~shmuel/L%5E2%20cohomology%20readings/Kammeyer%20Introduction%20To%20_%5E2-%20invariants.pdf, Introduction to L2-Invariants. Holger Kammeyer's textbook on $L^2$-Betti numbers, $L^2$-torsion, and von Neumann algebras.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2209.15039, Derived Kirwan Desingularization. Generalizing Kirwan's partial desingularization algorithm to derived Artin stacks with intrinsic stabilizer reduction.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/9901053, Quantum Toda Hamiltonians and Whittaker Functions. Etingof's paper computing quantum Toda Hamiltonians from Casimir actions on Whittaker functions on quantum groups.

https://www.math.columbia.edu/~woit/twistorp1.pdf, The Twistor Projective Line. Peter Woit on $\mathbb{P}^1$ in 4D Euclidean spacetime geometry and as the infinite prime counterpart of the Fargues–Fontaine curve.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2310.09972, Octonions and Split Octonions via Clifford Algebras. Algebraic construction of octonions, split octonions, and exceptional Jordan algebras.

https://www.math.uchicago.edu/~may/CONCISE/ConciseRevised.pdf, A Concise Course in Algebraic Topology. J.P. May's text covering fundamental groups, covering spaces, CW complexes, axiomatic homology, and fiber bundles.

https://www.math.uni-hamburg.de/home/richter/bookdraft.pdf, From Categories to Homotopy Theory. Birgit Richter's textbook covering categories, simplicial sets, nerve constructions, classifying spaces, and model categories.

https://perso.ens-lyon.fr/sophie.morel/2014_Spring_MorelClass.pdf, Global Langlands for Function Fields. Sophie Morel's course notes on adelic automorphic forms, Hecke algebras, and global Langlands parameters $\operatorname{Gal}(\overline{F}/F) \to \widehat{G}(\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_\ell)$.

https://youtu.be/qlGT6SGCdEc?si=56Ga4UqCM_mJBkHD, The Continuous Spectrum of Eisenstein Series. Video lecture on the spectral decomposition of the Laplacian and the continuous spectrum of Eisenstein series.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1103.5628, Introduction to Vassiliev Knot Invariants. Expository introduction to finite type knot invariants, chord diagrams, and the Kontsevich integral.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.01024v1, Inequalities on Markov Decision Processes. Analytical inequalities and probabilistic bounds in discrete Markov processes.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1011.2462, Tropical Semifields and Langlands Duality. Duality of Whittaker functions and representations over tropical semifields.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1202.4377, Universal Thickening of the Real Numbers. Noncommutative geometry and arithmetic geometry on thickenings of $\mathbb{R}$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0212126, Vertex Coordinates of the Associahedron. Explicit combinatorial formulas for the vertices of Stasheff associahedra and Tamari posets.

https://math.mit.edu/~apost/papers/permutohedron.pdf, Geometry of the Permutohedron. Marcelo Aguiar's notes on the geometry, braid arrangements, and Hopf algebra structures of the permutohedron.

https://virtualmath1.stanford.edu/~ralph/math215b/Bredon.pdf, Topology and Geometry by Glen Bredon. Classic textbook covering differential manifolds, de Rham cohomology, singular homology, and the Poincaré duality theorem.

https://www.kurims.kyoto-u.ac.jp/~piotr/252y_notes.pdf, Chromatic Homotopy and Formal Groups. 110-page notes on stable homotopy theory, formal group laws, Lubin–Tate theory, Chern classes, and the classification of thick subcategories.

https://www.claymath.org/library/academy/LectureNotes05/Lodaypaper.pdf, The Associahedron: Geometry, Combinatorics and Algebra. Jean-Louis Loday's survey on the Stasheff polytope $K_n$, Tamari order, planar binary trees, dendriform algebras, and moduli spaces $\mathcal{M}_{0,n}(\mathbb{R})$.

https://webusers.imj-prg.fr/~leila.schneps/MSRI.pdf, Mapping Class Groups and Grothendieck-Teichmuller Theory. Leila Schneps on arithmetic homomorphisms between mapping class groups in genus 0 and 1.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~serganov/ilyaz.org/papers/Reciprocity_Langlands/Langlands-pictorial-20221020.pdf, Visual Guide to Reciprocity and Langlands. Inna Zakharevich's pictorial introduction to quadratic reciprocity and Langlands functoriality.

https://jvoight.github.io/quat-book-v1.0.5.pdf, Quaternion Algebras by John Voight. Comprehensive 800+ page textbook on quaternion algebras, arithmetic Fuchsian groups, and Shimura curves.

https://math.mit.edu/~poonen/papers/Qpoints.pdf, Rational Points on Varieties. Bjorn Poonen's book on Diophantine geometry, local-global principles, and the Brauer–Manin obstruction.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~fengt/263C.pdf, Akshay Venkatesh's Lectures on L-Functions. Tony Feng's notes covering the subconvexity problem, periods, and automorphic forms.

https://indico.global/event/9583/attachments/42067/78761/slides_Bismut.pdf, Quillen Metrics and Hypoelliptic Laplacians. Jean-Michel Bismut's slides on analytic torsion, hypoelliptic Laplacians, and geometric index theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.17778, Topology of Lefschetz Pencils. Topological degeneration of smooth algebraic varieties, vanishing cycles, and Picard–Lefschetz theory.

https://www.math.stonybrook.edu/~kirillov/mat552/liegroups.pdf, Lie Groups and Lie Algebras by Alexander Kirillov. Comprehensive lecture notes covering matrix Lie groups, root systems, and representation theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2312.05922, Configuration Space Integrals and Chern-Simons Theory. Integrals over configuration spaces of trivalent ribbon graphs and generalized perturbative Chern–Simons invariants.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2411.07169, Topological Criteria for Moduli Stacks. Measuring how far an algebraic stack is from being a scheme and applications to principal bundles on curves.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/hep-th/0610328, K-Theory Classification of D-Branes. Survey of Sen conjecture proposals, tachyon condensation, and topological $K$-theory classifications of D-branes.

https://florianadler.github.io/inftyCats/inftyCats.pdf, Introduction to Infinity-Categories. Lecture notes covering quasicategories, Kan complexes, and $\infty$-categorical limits.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0703392, The Noncommutative Geometry of the Adele Class Space. Alain Connes on the spectral interpretation of the zeros of the Riemann zeta function via the adèle class space.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1602.06674, Comparing Sheaf and Singular Cohomology. Criteria under which singular cohomology and sheaf cohomology coincide for semi-locally contractible and paracompact spaces.

https://www.sci.utah.edu/~hbhatia/pubs/2013_TVCG_survey.pdf, Helmholtz-Hodge Decomposition Survey. Survey on decomposing vector fields on Riemannian manifolds into divergence-free, curl-free, and harmonic components.

Andrej Bauer's slides on Lawvere's categorical fixed-point theorem: if $e: A \to B^A$ is surjective, then every $f: B \to B$ has a fixed point. Unifies Cantor's theorem, Russell's paradox, Gödel's incompleteness, and the Halting problem.

https://danielnaylor.uk/notes/III/Easter/SGT/SGT.pdf, Spectral Graph Theory Notes. 43-page Cambridge Part III notes covering graph Laplacians, Cheeger inequalities, expanders, and random walks.

https://www.ggi.infn.it/talkfiles/slides/slides6640.pdf, Higher Order Jet Functors in Noncommutative Geometry. 22 slides on first-order differential calculi, universal derivations, and Spencer cohomology.

https://www.ingo-blechschmidt.eu/, Ingo Blechschmidt's Topos Theory Archive. Research repository on internal languages of topoi, constructive algebraic geometry, and synthetic mathematics.

https://rawgit.quasicoherent.io/iblech/internal-methods/master/slides-pssl104.pdf, Flabby Sets in Topos Theory. Presentation on flasque sheaves and the internal perspective in sheaf topoi.

https://rawgit.quasicoherent.io/iblech/internal-methods/master/slides-edinburgh2019.pdf, Generalized Nullstellensatz in Topoi. 26 slides on the constructive and topos-theoretic Nullstellensatz.

https://rawgit.quasicoherent.io/iblech/internal-methods/master/slides-unilog2018.pdf, Sheaf Semantics and Kripke-Joyal Logic. Exploring how classical theorems (like the intermediate value theorem) behave constructively in sheaf topoi via Kripke–Joyal semantics.

https://rawgit.quasicoherent.io/iblech/internal-methods/master/slides-como2018.pdf, The Petit and Gros Zariski Toposes. Treating schemes as plain sets and sheaves of modules as plain modules in the internal language of the Zariski topos.

https://rawgit.quasicoherent.io/iblech/internal-methods/master/slides-leipzig2018.pdf, Microaffinity in Synthetic Differential Geometry.

https://rawgit.quasicoherent.io/iblech/internal-methods/master/notes.pdf, Internal Methods in Algebraic Geometry. Ingo Blechschmidt's PhD thesis exploring the internal language of sheaf topoi (specifically the petit and gros Zariski topoi) to rephrase and prove theorems in algebraic geometry using intuitionistic commutative algebra. Details the dictionary between internal ring-theoretic statements and external geometric properties of schemes.

https://c-keyes.github.io/files/definitions.pdf, Comprehensive Glossary of Algebraic Geometry. A 46-page reference dictionary compiling core definitions and theorems:
- **Abelian Categories & Additivity:** Categories equipped with biproducts $\bigoplus$, kernels, cokernels, and the first isomorphism theorem $\operatorname{Coim}(f) \cong \operatorname{Im}(f)$. Semiadditive categories satisfy $\coprod \cong \prod$, yielding semiring structures on endomorphism sets $\operatorname{End}(X)$.
- **Ampleness & Positivity:** A line bundle $\mathcal{L}$ on a projective scheme $X$ is very ample if it admits a closed immersion $X \hookrightarrow \mathbb{P}_A^n$ defined by global sections; ample if $\mathcal{L}^{\otimes m}$ is very ample for some $m > 0$. Base-point freeness means global sections generate the stalks $\mathcal{L}_x$ everywhere.
- **Divisors & Canonical Sheaves:** Cartier divisors are global sections of $\mathcal{K}_X^\times / \mathcal{O}_X^\times$. Weil divisors $\sum n_i Y_i$ are formal integer linear combinations of codimension-1 prime subvarieties. The canonical bundle $\omega_X = \det(\Omega_{X/k}^1)$ is the top exterior power of Kähler differentials, acting as the dualizing sheaf in Serre duality.
- **Morphisms & Flatness:** Flat morphisms preserve exact sequences under tensor products $\mathcal{F} \otimes_{\mathcal{O}_Y} \mathcal{O}_X$, ensuring constant Hilbert polynomials across fibers. Étale morphisms are smooth of relative dimension 0 (flat and unramified). Proper morphisms are separated, finite type, and universally closed.
- **Cohomology & Derived Functors:** Sheaf cohomology groups $H^i(X, \mathcal{F}) = R^i\Gamma(X, \mathcal{F})$ quantify obstructions to gluing local sections into global ones. Flasque and soft sheaves are acyclic, computing Čech cohomology without higher derived corrections.

https://www.cantabgold.net/users/s.sasaki.03/Hida-Coleman.pdf, Hida and Coleman Families of Modular Forms. Kevin Buzzard's lecture notes on $p$-adic modular forms, ordinary families, Hida families, and the Coleman eigencurve.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/show/Mitchell-B%C3%A9nabou+language, The Mitchell-Benabou Language. Category-theoretic logic interpreting higher-order intuitionistic predicate logic inside elementary topoi using subobject classifiers $\Omega$, power objects, and Heyting algebras.

https://hal.science/hal-03606892v1/file/EnrichCat_pour_HAL.pdf, Short Introduction to Enriched Categories. Francis Borceux's 21-page primer covering categories enriched over monoidal categories $\mathcal{V}$, $\mathcal{V}$-functors, and $\mathcal{V}$-natural transformations.

http://www.staff.science.uu.nl/~ooste110/syllabi/toposmoeder.pdf, Topos Theory and Presheaf Logic. Ieke Moerdijk's 16-page lecture notes on presheaves, sites, Grothendieck topoi, and intuitionistic logic.

https://www.cantabgold.net/users/s.sasaki.03/LNTG_zoom_10_06_2020_B.pdf, Modularity Conjectures and Galois Representations. Slides on modularity conjectures, deformation rings $R = T$, and Galois representations.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/FinsterTypesAndOpetopes2012.pdf, Type Theory and Opetopic Categories. Eric Finster's notes on opetopes, multitopes, and the formalization of higher-dimensional categories in type theory.

https://www.math.columbia.edu/~goldfeld/TraceFormulae-4-12-2020.pdf, Explicit Trace Formulas for SL(2, R). Dorian Goldfeld's lecture notes providing a catalog of Kuznetsov, Petersson, and Selberg trace formulas on $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{R})$.

https://github.com/dmzb/dmzb.github.io/tree/main/slides, David Zureick-Brown's Arithmetic Geometry Slides. Archive of lecture slides covering Chabauty–Coleman methods, rational points on curves, and Diophantine geometry.

https://math.bu.edu/people/xyz6/intersection.pdf, The Splitting Principle and Chern Classes. Notes on the splitting principle: reducing calculations of Chern classes of higher-rank vector bundles to formal sums of line bundle classes.

https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VUzCOnZgtvI, Artin Reciprocity via Spheres. Dustin Clausen's lecture formulating the Artin reciprocity map via algebraic $K$-theory for fields $F$ with virtual cohomological dimension $\operatorname{vcd}_\ell(F) \le 2$. Highlights the isomorphism $C_F \cong \pi_1 K(\mathbf{lc}_F)$ on locally compact $F$-modules and the universal class in $\pi_0 d_K K(\mathbf{lc}_{\mathbb{Z}[1/\ell]})$.

https://www.math.ens.psl.eu/~obenoist/refs/Silverman.pdf, Arithmetic of Diophantine Equations. Joseph Silverman's notes on solving Diophantine equations by analyzing reductions modulo $p$, lifting solutions via Hensel's lemma in $\mathbb{Q}_p$, and applying the Hasse local-to-global principle.

https://ebooks.karbust.me/Mathematics/Joseph%20H.%20Silverman%20-%20Advanced%20Topics%20in%20the%20Arithmetic%20of%20Elliptic%20Curves%20(1994)%20[978-1-4612-0851-8].pdf, Advanced Topics in the Arithmetic of Elliptic Curves. Joseph Silverman's classic text covering complex multiplication, Néron models, Tate curves, modular curves, and Galois representations.

https://math.mit.edu/classes/18.783/2025/links.html, MIT Course on Elliptic Curves. Course materials and lecture notes by Andrew Sutherland on arithmetic of elliptic curves, point counting, and modularity.

https://math.mit.edu/classes/18.783/2025/LectureSlides1.pdf, Introduction to Elliptic Curves. Sutherland's introductory slides on smooth projective curves of genus 1, Weierstrass equations $y^2 = x^3 + Ax + B$, the chord-and-tangent group law, Mordell's theorem $E(\mathbb{Q}) \cong T \oplus \mathbb{Z}^r$, the Sato–Tate conjecture, and the BSD conjecture.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.07136, Birational Motives. Construction of birational motivic categories and homotopy invariants of algebraic varieties.

https://www.claymath.org/library/monographs/cmim02.pdf, Motivic Cohomology and the Milnor Conjecture. Monograph on Voevodsky's proof of the Milnor conjecture, motivic complexes, and $\mathbb{A}^1$-homotopy theory.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~avizeff/seminars/motivic-cohomology-talk.pdf, Distillation of Motivic Cohomology. Seminar notes on motivic complexes $\mathbb{Z}(n)$, the Bloch–Kato conjecture, and comparison with étale cohomology.

https://ctnt-summer.math.uconn.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/1632/2016/02/Xiao.pdf, Quaternion Algebras and Reciprocity. Liang Xiao's notes on quaternion algebras, Hilbert symbols, and local-global reciprocity.

https://documentserver.uhasselt.be/bitstream/1942/23769/2/Octonions5.pdf, Octonions and Exceptional Lie Algebras. Notes on the Cayley–Dickson construction, the octonion algebra $\mathbb{O}$, the automorphism group $G_2$, and the Freudenthal magic square.

https://people.maths.ox.ac.uk/horawa/math_631.pdf, Mircea Mustata's Algebraic Geometry. 200+ page comprehensive lecture notes covering affine/projective varieties, sheaves, morphisms, and dimension theory.

https://weilab.math.msu.edu/CTGG/slides/internal-online-seminar-lect1.pdf, Overview of Algebraic Topology. 26 slides reviewing fundamental groups, covering spaces, and singular homology.

https://pi.math.cornell.edu/~barbasch/china2024.pdf, Dirac Operators and Representation Theory. Dan Barbasch's 47 slides on the Dirac operator $D = \sum \rho(X_i) \otimes \gamma_i$, Clifford algebras, and Dirac cohomology in the unitary representation theory of reductive Lie groups.

Hisham Sati's slides on flux quantization, exceptional holonomy, and differential cohomology in M-theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1407.3945, Cyclic and Epicyclic Sites. Alain Connes and Caterina Consani on the cyclic and epicyclic categories, cyclic cohomology, and the arithmetic site.

https://personalpages.manchester.ac.uk/staff/Nicola.Gambino/pssl-101/Blechschmidt.pdf, Internal Language of Topoi in Algebraic Geometry. Ingo Blechschmidt's slides on the petit and gros Zariski topoi:
- **Petit Zariski Topos $\operatorname{Shv}(X)$:** The structure sheaf $\mathcal{O}_X$ internally behaves as a field (or local ring where every non-invertible element is nilpotent). A sheaf of modules $\mathcal{F}$ is locally of finite type if and only if it is internally finitely generated. Coherent sheaves correspond internally to finitely presented modules.
- **Gros Zariski Topos $\operatorname{Zar}(S)$:** Represents schemes by their functors of points $\mathbf{Sch}/S \to \mathbf{Set}$. The affine line $\mathbb{A}^1$ behaves internally as a commutative field where every function $\mathbb{A}^1 \to \mathbb{A}^1$ is a polynomial. Group schemes $\mathbb{G}_a, \mathbb{G}_m, \mathrm{GL}_n$ appear internally as standard algebraic groups.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1004.3802, Stack Semantics and Internal Topos Languages. Mike Shulman's paper extending Kripke–Joyal semantics to higher stacks and categorical fibrations.

https://pi.math.cornell.edu/~dkmiller/bin/almost-cotangent.pdf, The Almost Cotangent Complex. 5-page notes on the cotangent complex of perfectoid rings and Faltings' almost purity theorem.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2409.01940, Derived Localization and Idempotent Ideals. Derived categories of modules over idempotent ideals and localization in positive characteristic.

https://www.math.cmu.edu/~af1p/Talks/RandomGraphs/rgtalk.pdf, Survey of Random Graphs. 114 slides by Alan Frieze surveying threshold functions, giant components, graph colorings, and random regular graphs.

https://math.gmu.edu/~rsachs/talks/maastirlingbeamer.pdf, Stirling's Formula and Applications. Slides deriving Stirling's approximation $n! \sim \sqrt{2\pi n}(n/e)^n$ via trapezoidal integration of $\ln x$ and central limit approximations.

https://www.sisask.com/Shillong/DFA_2.pdf, Discrete Harmonic Analysis and Gowers Norms. Notes on characters $\chi: G \to S^1$, Fourier inversion, character orthogonality, and Gowers uniformity norms.

https://people.math.osu.edu/bergelson.1/ertupdatenov6.pdf, Ergodic Ramsey Theory. Vitaly Bergelson's monograph on multiple recurrence theorems, IP sets, and ultrafilters in combinatorial number theory.

https://www.math.clemson.edu/~macaule/classes/m23_math4340/index.html, Applied Engineering Mathematics. Matthew Macauley's lecture archive covering PDEs, Fourier series, and boundary value problems.

https://www.math.wustl.edu/~matkerr/5333/index.html, Algebraic Curves Course Archive. Matt Kerr's course on compact Riemann surfaces, divisors, the Riemann–Roch theorem, and Abel's theorem.

https://www.mathematik.uni-muenchen.de/~wehler/Talk_Summary.pdf, Harmonic Theory on Compact Riemann Surfaces. 17 slides on the Hodge star operator $*$, harmonic 1-forms as the kernel of the Laplacian $\Delta = d\delta + \delta d$, the Hodge decomposition $H^1_{\text{dR}}(X) \cong \mathcal{H}^1(X)$, and Dolbeault cohomology.

https://web.stanford.edu/~restrin/files/refresher.pdf, Linear Algebra Refresher. 117-slide quick review covering vector spaces, linear transformations, inner products, eigenvalues, and SVD.

https://idv.sinica.edu.tw/ftliang/diff_geom/*diff_geometry(II)/3.11/laplacian_2.pdf, The Laplace-Beltrami Operator on Differential Forms. Detailed notes on the Hodge Laplacian $\Delta = d\delta + \delta d$ on oriented compact Riemannian manifolds and harmonic forms.

https://indico.math.cnrs.fr/event/5382/attachments/2589/3779/Pandharipande_Course.pdf, Enumerative Geometry of Curves, Maps, and Sheaves. Rahul Pandharipande's 202 slides covering moduli of nodal curves $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}$, deformation theory ($\operatorname{Def}(C) \cong H^1(T_C)$), Gromov–Witten theory, and Hodge integrals.

https://www.cs.utexas.edu/~EWD/transcriptions/EWD10xx/EWD1094.html, Dijkstra on the Pigeonhole Principle. Edsger W. Dijkstra's note reformulating the pigeonhole principle: in any non-empty finite bag of real numbers, the maximum is at least the average and the minimum is at most the average.

https://matthewhirn.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/04/cmse890_spring2021_all-lectures.pdf, Spectral Graph Theory Lecture Notes. 155-page notes on graph Laplacians, Cheeger's inequality, spectral clustering, and diffusion maps.

https://cs.uwaterloo.ca/~lapchi/cs860-2025/ASGT.pdf, Advanced Spectral Graph Theory. Lap Chi Lau's 288-page draft textbook covering electrical networks, effective resistances, random walks, and graph sparsification.

https://web.stanford.edu/class/cs168/l/l11.pdf, Spectral Graph Theory in 6 Pages. Concise introduction to the normalized graph Laplacian, Rayleigh quotients, and spectral partitioning.

https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/18-706-noncommutative-algebra-spring-2023/mit18_706_s23_full_lec.pdf, Noncommutative Algebra Course Notes. MIT OpenCourseWare notes covering central simple algebras, the Brauer group, Morita equivalence, and the Jacobson radical.

https://mathoverflow.net/questions/253010/why-study-higher-sheaf-cohomology/253051#253051, Why Higher Sheaf Cohomology Matters. Will Sawin's explanation of higher cohomology groups as classification invariants and their role in Dolbeault cohomology.

https://mathoverflow.net/questions/201853/why-is-differential-galois-theory-not-widely-used/201859#201859, Applications of Differential Galois Theory. Discussion on differential Galois theory, Picard–Vessiot extensions, and why differential equations are often solved via geometric analysis instead.

https://mathoverflow.net/questions/263223/arithmetic-morse-theory/263224#263224, Arithmetic Morse Theory and Lefschetz Pencils. Connecting Morse theory on smooth manifolds to Lefschetz pencils, vanishing cycles, and Picard–Lefschetz monodromy in algebraic geometry.

https://mathoverflow.net/questions/344746/smooth-vs-regular-vs-non-singular/344749#344749, Smoothness vs Regularity vs Non-Singularity. Clarifies definitions: regularity is an intrinsic stalk property ($\dim \mathcal{O}_{X,x} = \dim \mathfrak{m}/\mathfrak{m}^2$), whereas smoothness is a relative property of scheme morphisms (flat, finite presentation, geometrically regular fibers).

https://sanathdevalapurkar.github.io/files/calculus-and-cohomology.pdf, Geometric Satake and Calculus. Sanath Devalapurkar's notes exploring the Geometric Satake Equivalence via differential forms and chiral homology.

https://pi.math.cornell.edu/~erin/analysis/lectures.pdf, Proof of Holder's Inequality. Elementary proof of Young's inequality $ab \le a^p/p + b^q/q$ and its deduction of Hölder's inequality for $L^p$ spaces.

https://pi.math.cornell.edu/~erin/docs/distributions_oh.pdf, Introduction to Distribution Theory. Primer on test functions $C_c^\infty(\Omega)$, distributions $\mathcal{D}'(\Omega)$, weak derivatives, and the Dirac delta.

https://lebras.perso.math.cnrs.fr/Notes_workshop_all.pdf, Arakelov Geometry Workshop Notes. Lecture notes covering arithmetic surfaces, Green functions, Arakelov intersection theory, and Faltings' height functions.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0907.4226, The Madsen-Weiss Theorem for Topologists. Expository introduction to the Madsen–Weiss theorem on the stable moduli space of Riemann surfaces and cobordism categories.

https://www.math.uchicago.edu/~may/EXTHEORY/MaySig.pdf, Parametrized Homotopy Theory. May and Sigurdsson's 340-page monograph on parameterized spaces, parameterized spectra, and duality in stable homotopy theory.

https://web.stanford.edu/~dkim04/writings/notes/spencer-probabilistic-methods.pdf, Spencer's Probabilistic Methods Notes. Notes on non-constructive existence proofs: if the probability of the bad event is strictly less than 1, a valid configuration exists.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2504.15542, Igusa Stacks and Shimura Varieties. Geometry of Igusa stacks in the reduction of Shimura varieties and Galois representations.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1107.6032, Fixed Point Theorems in Topology. Comprehensive survey of Brouwer, Lefschetz, Schauder, and Lawvere fixed-point theorems.

https://isft2023.sciencesconf.org/data/LTakhtajan.pdf, Heat Kernel Proof of Atiyah-Singer. Leon Takhtajan's slides on the McKean–Singer formula, supersymmetry, and the heat kernel proof of the index theorem.

https://math.stackexchange.com/questions/281807/two-definitions-of-limsup, Definitions of Limsup. Discussion clarifying $\limsup x_n = \inf_{k} \sup_{n \ge k} x_n$ as the greatest subsequential limit.

https://runegha.folk.ntnu.no/naivecat_web.pdf, Naive Infinity-Categories. Rune Haugseng's lecture notes covering simplicial sets, quasicategories, mapping spaces, and joins.

https://www.math.univ-paris13.fr/~harpaz/thurnau_talk.pdf, Etale Homotopy and the Local-Global Principle. Slides on the Hasse principle for varieties over number fields, Brauer–Manin obstructions, and étale homotopy fixed points $X(\overline{K})^{hG_K}$.

https://www.math.utoronto.ca/~ila/Cox-Primes_of_the_form_x2+ny2.pdf, Primes of the Form x^2 + ny^2. David A. Cox's classic text bridging quadratic forms, class field theory, and complex multiplication of elliptic curves.

https://www.math.univ-paris13.fr/~harpaz/modular_forms.pdf, Modular Curves and Modular Forms. 17-page notes covering $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$, congruence subgroups $\Gamma_0(N)$, Eisenstein series, and cusp forms.
