https://cel.hal.science/cel-00963631v2/document, Roth's Theorem and Regularity Methods. Survey on proving Roth's theorem on 3-term APs via discrete Fourier analysis and triangle removal lemmas.

https://www.sayantankhan.io/pages/pdfs/notes/roth.pdf, Roth's Theorem via the Circle Method. Notes detailing the proof of Roth's theorem using exponential sums and density increment arguments.

https://philpuetzstueck.gitlab.io/documents/sheaves-on-manifolds.pdf, Sheaves on Manifolds and Six Functors. Comprehensive treatise expanding on the six-functor formalism for sheaves on topological manifolds via condensed mathematics.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/foliationsfine.pdf, Noncommutative Geometry of Foliations. Alain Connes' survey on leaf spaces of foliations, longitudinal index theorems, and von Neumann algebras.

https://homepages.math.uic.edu/~henri/notes/Morelia_slides.pdf, Arithmetic Chow Groups and Arakelov Theory. Henri Gillet's 81 slides on arithmetic Chow groups $\widehat{\operatorname{CH}}^p(X)$, Green currents, and the arithmetic Riemann–Roch theorem.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/The-scaling-site-2016.pdf, The Scaling Site. Alain Connes and Caterina Consani's paper constructing the scaling site as a semiringed topos $([0, \infty) \rtimes \mathbb{N}^\times, \mathcal{O})$. Points over the tropical semifield $\mathbb{R}_{\max}$ recover the adèle class space $X_{\mathbb{Q}} = \mathbb{Q}^\times \backslash \mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{Q}} / \widehat{\mathbb{Z}}^\times$, providing an arithmetic model for the Riemann zeros.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/GeomoverF1.pdf, Geometry over F1 and the Riemann Hypothesis. Connes and Consani on Arakelov geometry, Chevalley group schemes over $\mathbb{F}_1$, root systems, and the spectral interpretation of zeta zeros.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0404185, Introduction to F1 Geometry. Survey on the field with one element $\mathbb{F}_1$, monoid schemes, and the combinatorial limits of $\mathbb{F}_q$-varieties as $q \to 1$.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/Qlattices-1.pdf, Quantum Statistical Mechanics of Q-Lattices. Connes and Marcolli on 1- and 2-dimensional $\mathbb{Q}$-lattices, the Bost–Connes system, modular curves, and phase transitions.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/whySM.pdf, The Standard Model from Spectral Geometry. Alain Connes on deriving the $U(1) \times \mathrm{SU}(2) \times \mathrm{SU}(3)$ gauge group and the Higgs mechanism from an almost-commutative spectral triple of KO-dimension 6 modulo 8.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/poincareseminar2007.pdf, Noncommutative Geometry and Physics. Poincare Seminar paper on noncommutative geometry, Dirac operators $D$, line elements $ds = D^{-1}$, inner automorphisms $\operatorname{Inn}(\mathcal{A})$, and spectral actions.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/bookwebfinal-2.pdf, Noncommutative Geometry Monograph. Alain Connes' 600+ page foundational textbook covering:
- **Operator Algebras & Factors:** Classification of Type I, II, and III von Neumann factors. Type II factors exhibit continuous dimensions.
- **Time Evolution & the KMS Condition:** The Tomita–Takesaki theorem and the canonical homomorphism $\delta: \mathbb{R} \to \operatorname{Out}(M) = \operatorname{Aut}(M)/\operatorname{Inn}(M)$ showing that noncommutativity naturally generates time evolution.
- **Foliations & Cyclic Cohomology:** Leaf spaces as noncommutative spaces, longitudinal index theorems, cyclic cohomology $HC^*(A)$, and noncommutative de Rham homology.
- **Quantum Mechanics & Uncertainty:** Noncommuting variables $[P, Q] = i\hbar$, spectral geometry, Dirac operators as square roots of Laplacians, and Dixmier traces.

https://mathoverflow.net/questions/386464/making-sense-of-every-non-commutative-algebra-has-its-own-internal-time-evoluti, Internal Time Evolution in Operator Algebras. Dmitri Pavlov's explanation of the modular automorphism group in Tomita–Takesaki theory as a canonical 1-parameter group of outer automorphisms.

https://archive.ymsc.tsinghua.edu.cn/pacm_download/343/11610-Zhengwei_Liu_4.pdf, The Noncommutative Uncertainty Principle. Zhengwei Liu's slides on subfactor theory, planar algebras, and quantum uncertainty relations.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/walkNCGgarden.pdf, A Walk in the Noncommutative Garden. Alain Connes' expository overview of spectral triples, quantum spaces, and the Riemann hypothesis.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/char1absolutepoint.pdf, Mathematics in Characteristic One. Connes and Consani on idempotent analysis over the tropical semifield $\mathbb{R}_+^{\max}$, Frobenius automorphisms $x \mapsto x^\lambda$, and the geometry of the absolute point $\operatorname{Spec} \mathbb{F}_1$.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/cmr0.pdf, KMS States and Complex Multiplication. Connes, Marcolli, and Ramachandran on the Bost–Connes system for imaginary quadratic fields and class field theory via modular functions.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/arithmeticsite.pdf, The Arithmetic Site. Connes and Consani on the presheaf topos of $\mathbb{N}^\times$ endowed with the structure sheaf of tropical integers $\mathbb{Z}_{\max}$.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/scalingsite.pdf, The Scaling Site and Zeta Zeros. Monograph on the scaling site, rank-1 subgroups of $\mathbb{R}$, and the geometric realization of the Frobenius flow.

https://agag-gathmann.math.rptu.de/class/alggeom-2021/alggeom-2021-c16.pdf, Computations in Sheaf Cohomology. Andreas Gathmann's lecture notes on computing cohomology of projective spaces $\mathbb{P}^n$ and Serre's vanishing theorems.

https://seohong.me/notes/Serre_Duality.pdf, Serre Duality in 6 Pages. Concise proof of Serre duality $H^i(X, \mathcal{F}) \cong H^{n-i}(X, \omega_X \otimes \mathcal{F}^*)^\vee$ for smooth projective varieties.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/SymmetricSpectra.pdf, Introduction to Symmetric Spectra. Alexander Kupers' summary of symmetric spectra, stable model structures, Day convolution, and smash products.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/1809.02944-3.pdf, Entropy and the Spectral Action. Connes and Consani on thermodynamic entropy, KMS states, and spectral action asymptotics.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/NCGspectral.pdf, Spectral Viewpoint on Noncommutative Geometry. Survey on the spectral paradigm: replacing Riemannian metrics with Dirac operators, fermion propagators, and KO-homology.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/conceptualSM.pdf, The Noncommutative Standard Model. Derivation of the full Standard Model Lagrangian coupled to gravity from the spectral action on an almost-commutative space.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/BC-system-absoute-cyclotomypublished.pdf, The Bost-Connes System and Absolute Cyclotomy. Phase transitions, spontaneous symmetry breaking with Galois group $\operatorname{Gal}(\mathbb{Q}^{\text{ab}}/\mathbb{Q}) \cong \widehat{\mathbb{Z}}^\times$, and $L$-functions.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/JAG2020-1.pdf, Algebraic Geometry over Segal's Gamma-Rings. Connes and Consani formulating algebraic geometry over the sphere spectrum $\mathbb{S}$ and compactifications of $\operatorname{Spec} \mathbb{Z}$.

https://ziyangzhu.github.io/home/Arakelov.pdf, Concise Course in Arakelov Geometry. Notes on arithmetic Chow groups, Hermitian metrics, Green currents, and the arithmetic Riemann–Roch theorem.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/CamaraOnArakelov11.pdf, Overview of Arakelov Theory. 18-page introduction to arithmetic surfaces, Deligne pairings, and Faltings' height functions.

https://www.maths.tcd.ie/~btyrrel/flatness.pdf, Intuitive Guide to Flatness. Visual guide to flat families of schemes, constant Hilbert polynomials, and the absence of dimension jumping in fibers.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~lurie/252xnotes/Lecture18.pdf, The Landweber Exact Functor Theorem. Jacob Lurie's lecture notes proving the Landweber exact functor theorem: classifying complex oriented homology theories flat over $\mathcal{M}_{\text{fg}}$.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/reality.pdf, Noncommutativity and Reality. Alain Connes' essay on quantum reality, operator algebras, and the emergence of spacetime.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/thermodynamicsofendomotives.pdf, Thermodynamics of Endomotives. Connes, Consani, and Marcolli on endomotives, thermodynamic semirings, and arithmetic Bost–Connes systems.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/On-the-Metaphysics-of-F_12024-published.pdf, On the Metaphysics of F1. Philosophical and mathematical reflections on the field with one element and the Riemann hypothesis.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/ncgphysics.pdf, Noncommutative Geometry and Fundamental Physics. Alain Connes' landmark survey detailing:
- **Quantum Variables:** Real variables correspond to self-adjoint operators in Hilbert space; infinitesimals correspond to compact operators.
- **Metric Structures:** Distance formula $d(p, q) = \sup \{|f(p) - f(q)| : \|[D, f]\| \le 1\}$, where the line element $ds = D^{-1}$ is the fermion propagator.
- **Thermodynamics & Time:** Noncommutativity implies a canonical 1-parameter automorphism group $\delta_t \in \operatorname{Out}(\mathcal{A})$ via the Tomita–Takesaki KMS condition.
- **Bost-Connes System:** Exhibits spontaneous symmetry breaking at critical temperature $\beta = 1$ with the Riemann zeta function $\zeta(\beta)$ as the partition function.

https://celebratio.org/connes_a/article/842/, In-Depth Interview with Alain Connes. Biographical interview on the discovery of Type III factor classifications, cyclic cohomology, and the spectral action.

https://repo-archives.ihes.fr/FONDS_IHES/I_Prepublications/CONNES/Depuis_1999/M_99_75/M_99_75.pdf, Renormalization and the Riemann-Hilbert Problem. Connes and Kreimer's paper establishing that perturbative renormalization in QFT is a Riemann–Hilbert problem on the motivic Galois group of Feynman graphs.

https://www.math.fsu.edu/~marcolli/KITPslides.pdf, Hopf Algebras and Renormalization. Matilde Marcolli's slides on the Connes–Kreimer Hopf algebra of rooted trees and dimensional regularization.

https://alainconnes.org/wp-content/uploads/geometryquantumCCM.pdf, The Quantum Space-Time Paradigm. Chamseddine, Connes, and Mukhanov on quantum gravity, spectral actions, and geometry from operator algebras.

https://www.math.ku.dk/english/calendar/events/masterclass-continuous-k-theory/Clausen1.pdf, Deligne Cohomology and Continuous K-Theory. Dustin Clausen's masterclass slides on Deligne cohomology, arithmetic regulators, and continuous algebraic $K$-theory.

https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=69-_otYjClg, Geometry of the Adele Class Space: Part I. Alain Connes' lecture exploring:
- **Number Fields & Schemes:** Unifying number rings $\mathcal{O}_K$, function fields $\mathbb{F}_q(C)$, and Riemann surfaces as 1-dimensional regular schemes.
- **Galois Theory & Etale Topology:** Separable algebras, traces, finite étale morphisms, and why $\operatorname{Spec} \mathbb{Z}$ behaves as a simply connected 3-manifold where primes are knots.
- **Topos Semantics:** Topoi allow groups/monoids to act naturally. The topos $([0, \infty) \rtimes \mathbb{N}^\times)$ computes points over the Boolean semifield $\mathbb{B} = \{0,1\}$, recovering the adèle class space.

https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XPVfElqM264, Geometry of the Adele Class Space: Part II. Alain Connes' second lecture:
- **Adèles & Global Fields:** Global fields sit discretely and cocompactly in their locally compact adèle rings $\mathbb{A}_K$.
- **Trace Formulas & Absorption Spectra:** The scaling action on $K_v$ has Schwartz kernel $\delta(y - \lambda x)$ with trace $\int \delta(x - \lambda x)\,dx = 1/|1-\lambda|$. Zeros of $L$-functions emerge as an absorption spectrum, explaining the minus sign in explicit formulas.
- **The Arithmetic Site:** The topos $(\mathbb{N}^\times, \mathbb{Z}_{\max})$ over the tropical semifield $\mathbb{R}_{\max}$ exhibits Frobenius automorphisms $\operatorname{Fr}_\lambda(x) = x^\lambda$, establishing an explicit Riemann–Roch theorem for number fields.

https://web.math.ku.dk/~rordam/Masterclass-F13/Houdayer.pdf, Cartan Subalgebras and Von Neumann Algebras. Cyril Houdayer on Cartan decompositions and Type III factor classifications.

https://www.math.iitb.ac.in/~dprasad/dl-notes.pdf, Dipendra Prasad on Representation Theory. Comprehensive notes on reductive groups over finite fields, maximal tori, Harish-Chandra cusp forms, and Deligne–Lusztig induction.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~gross/preprints/gan-epilogue.pdf, Epilogue to the Gan-Gross-Prasad Conjectures. Benedict Gross on Bessel models, Fourier–Jacobi models, and automorphic periods.

https://www.kurims.kyoto-u.ac.jp/~kyodo/kokyuroku/contents/pdf/1571-04.pdf, Hida-Mazur Theory and Knots. Analogies between Alexander–Fox invariants in knot theory and Iwasawa modules in number theory.

https://www.mn.uio.no/math/personer/vit/rognes/papers/bergen10.pdf, Motivic Complexes and Rank Filtration. John Rognes on algebraic $K$-theory filtrations and motivic cohomology.

https://www.uio.no/studier/emner/matnat/math/MAT9580/gamle-undervisningsressurser/varen-2017/documents/spectra.pdf, Stable Homotopy Theory Course. John Rognes' lecture notes covering spectra, smash products, and localized ring spectra.

https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/iblech/talk-homological-algebra@master/notes-derived-functors.pdf, Quick Guide to Derived Functors. Ingo Blechschmidt's 16-page guide on computing right derived functors $R^i F$ via injective resolutions and long exact sequences.

https://www.uio.no/studier/emner/matnat/math/MAT9580/v23/documents/chromatic.pdf, Chromatic Homotopy Notes by Rognes. Advanced course notes on formal group laws, Morava $E$-theories, and the chromatic tower.

https://eddienijholt.nl/Picard-Lindelof.pdf, The Picard-Lindelof Theorem. Clean proof of the local existence and uniqueness of solutions to ODEs via Banach fixed-point iterations.

https://jenseberhardt.com/data/sheafcohomology.pdf, Sheaf Cohomology in 6 Pages. Jens Eberhardt's concise guide defining sheaf cohomology via derived categories, Ore localizations, and distinguished triangles.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/014P, Triangulated Categories in the Stacks Project. Foundational reference on distinguished triangles, rotation axioms, and the octahedral axiom.

https://www.math.lsu.edu/~pramod/tc/14s-7260/dercat.pdf, Derived Categories and Triangulations. Pramod Achar's lecture notes covering homotopy categories, mapping cones, and Verdier quotients.

https://mathoverflow.net/questions/3524/distinguished-triangles-and-cohomology, Distinguished Triangles and Cohomology. Discussion clarifying how short exact sequences in abelian categories lift to distinguished triangles in derived categories.

https://math.uchicago.edu/~may/REU2014/REUPapers/Peng.pdf, The Hahn-Banach Separation Theorem. Detailed exposition of geometric Hahn–Banach separation of convex bodies in topological vector spaces.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1407.5782, Gabber Points in Algebraic Geometry. Ofer Gabber's theorem on detecting isomorphisms in étale sheaf topoi via strictly Henselian local rings and fiber functors.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2208.07188, Locally Conformally Kahler Geometry. Ornea and Verbitsky on LCK manifolds, Lee forms, and Vaisman metric classifications.

http://verbit.ru/IMPA/Kahler-2025/, Misha Verbitsky's Kahler Geometry Course. 24 slide lectures covering complex manifolds, almost complex structures, Hermitian metrics, and Hodge theory.

http://verbit.ru/MATH/TALKS/positive-toric-2014.pdf, Principal Toric Fibrations. Verbitsky on positive toric fibrations and symplectic forms.

http://verbit.ru/MATH/TALKS/Israel/kahler-1.pdf, Complex Structures and Holonomy. Verbitsky's slides on $J^2 = -\operatorname{Id}$, Hodge decomposition $V_\mathbb{C} = V^{1,0} \oplus V^{0,1}$, Nijenhuis tensors, and Kähler forms.

http://verbit.ru/MATH/TALKS/Israel/kahler-2.pdf, Kahler Forms and Metric Geometry. Hermitian differential forms and the $\partial\overline{\partial}$-lemma.

http://verbit.ru/MATH/TALKS/Israel/kahler-3.pdf, Hermitian Riemannian Metrics. Connections, curvature tensors, and holonomy groups in complex geometry.

http://verbit.ru/MATH/TALKS/Israel/kahler-4.pdf, U(1)-Invariance in Complex Geometry. Weight decompositions under $U(1)$-actions on differential forms.

http://bogomolov-lab.ru/KURSY/GEOM-2013/, Differential Geometry Tutorials. Verbitsky's comprehensive 12-tutorial course covering smooth manifolds, connections, and Lie groups.

http://imperium.lenin.ru/~verbit/MATH/TALKS/alg-geom-H.pdf, Algebraic Geometry over the Quaternions. Verbitsky on hyperkähler manifolds, triholomorphic subvarieties, and quaternionic holonomy.

https://www.ipmu.jp/sites/default/files/webfm/pdfs/conference/Olmos_slides.pdf, Holonomy of Riemannian Manifolds. Carlos Olmos's 207 slides on Berger's holonomy classification, symmetric spaces, and transnormal systems.

https://faculty.tcu.edu/gfriedman/cbms2012/CBMSsingle.pdf, Griffiths on Hodge Theory and Moduli. 287-page CBMS lecture notes by Phillip Griffiths covering period maps, variations of Hodge structure, and monodromy.

https://projects.au.dk/fileadmin/mathweekend2013/Slides/EMS-Aarhus_Weiss.pdf, Holonomy Groups via Flow Methods. Slides on classifying Riemannian holonomy groups through geometric flows.

https://wis.kuleuven.be/events/archive/multisymplectic/slides/madsen-slides/@@download/file/Madsen.pdf, Toric Symplectic Geometry. Slides on Delzant's theorem classifying compact toric symplectic manifolds via Delzant polytopes.

https://www.math.uni-hamburg.de/projekte/dgsusy/Galaev%20Slides%20Part%203.pdf, Holonomy of Supermanifolds. Anton Galaev's slides on Berger-type holonomy classifications for pseudo-Riemannian supermanifolds.

https://sites.lsa.umich.edu/sdpayne/wp-content/uploads/sites/1450/2025/08/M23.pdf, Moduli of Curves of Genus 22 and 23. Sam Payne on proving that the moduli spaces $\mathcal{M}_{22}$ and $\mathcal{M}_{23}$ are of general type.

https://www.math.stonybrook.edu/~cschnell/mat545/, Complex Geometry Course Archive. Christian Schnell's course covering several complex variables, Dolbeault cohomology, and Kähler manifolds.

https://math.uchicago.edu/~amathew/kodaira.pdf, The Kodaira Vanishing Theorem. Akhil Mathew's notes on Kodaira vanishing $H^i(X, K_X \otimes L) = 0$ for $i > 0$ and ample line bundles $L$.

https://www.math.stonybrook.edu/~cschnell/pdf/notes/aussois.pdf, Variations of Hodge Structure. Christian Schnell's masterclass notes on degenerations of Hodge structures on punctured discs, Schmid's nilpotent orbit theorem, and $\mathfrak{sl}_2$-representations.

https://sites.math.northwestern.edu/~george/preprints/ggLecNotesq-Series/ggLecNotesq-Series.pdf, Minicourse on q-Series. George Gasper's lecture notes covering basic hypergeometric series, Jacobi theta functions, and $q$-identities.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1704.08406, Elliptic Hypergeometric Functions on Root Systems. Survey on elliptic gamma functions, elliptic shifted factorials, and lattice models in statistical mechanics.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1608.06161, Elliptic Hypergeometric Functions. 69-page monograph on multi-variable elliptic hypergeometric integrals and their symmetries.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~smarks/mod-forms-tutorial/mf-notes/nonintro.pdf, Conceptual Introduction to Modular Forms. 6-page tutorial viewing modular forms as sections of line bundles over the modular curve $Y = \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z}) \backslash \mathbb{H}$, Fourier $q$-expansions, and Hecke eigenforms.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1809.10907, Henri Cohen on Modular Forms. Introductory text covering modular transformations, Eisenstein series, cusp forms, and Hecke operators.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/zagier/files/tex/UtrechtLectures/UtBook.pdf, Modular Forms: The 1-2-3 of Modular Forms. Don Zagier's classic lectures covering elliptic modular forms, the $j$-invariant, Jacobi forms, and Hilbert modular forms.

https://www.dpmms.cam.ac.uk/~jat58/EMS.pdf, The Modularity Conjecture. Jack Thorne's survey on modularity lifting theorems from Wiles to modern developments over totally real and CM fields.

https://www.dpmms.cam.ac.uk/~jat58/congruences.pdf, Congruences of Modular Forms. Jack Thorne on congruences between modular forms, Eisenstein ideals, and deformation rings.

https://people.maths.bris.ac.uk/~matyd/Trieste2017/Thorne.pdf, Modularity Beyond Elliptic Curves. Lecture notes on potential modularity of Galois representations and Calabi–Yau varieties.

https://www.dpmms.cam.ac.uk/~jat58/cdm-template.pdf, Reciprocity and Symmetric Powers. Jack Thorne on establishing the automorphy of symmetric power representations $\operatorname{Sym}^n \rho_f$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1705.09221, Root Systems and Basic Hypergeometric Integrals. Studies multi-variable $q$-integrals associated with affine Lie algebras.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1912.12971, Theory of Elliptic Hypergeometric Integrals. Comprehensive survey on elliptic hypergeometric integrals and hyperbolic quantum field theories.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1808.03441, Quantum Groups and q-Special Functions. Connecting basic hypergeometric series to representations of quantum groups $U_q(\mathfrak{g})$.

https://www.math.colostate.edu/~pries/467/Judson12.pdf, Abstract Algebra: Theory and Applications. Thomas Judson's textbook covering groups, rings, fields, and Galois theory.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/coll-55.pdf, Motives and Renormalization in QFT. Matilde Marcolli on dimensional regularization, minimal subtraction, and the Connes–Kreimer motivic Galois group.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/MAT1314HS2019.html, Advanced Noncommutative Geometry. Marcolli's course archive on spectral triples, $C^*$-algebras, and mathematical physics.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/Ma191Winter2014.html, Motives and Periods Course Archive. Course on Grothendieck motives, periods, dg-categories, and Tannakian categories.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/download/topology.pdf, Topology in the Stacks Project. Foundational reference on Hausdorff spaces, compactness, spectral spaces, sobriety, and Stone–Čech compactifications.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~reb/courses/261/all.pdf, Lie Theory by Richard Borcherds. Comprehensive lecture notes covering matrix Lie groups, solvable/nilpotent Lie algebras, Engel's and Lie's theorems, the Campbell–Baker–Hausdorff formula, Poincare–Birkhoff–Witt, root systems, and Clifford algebras.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/hep-th/9808042, Renormalization, Hopf Algebras and NCG. Connes and Kreimer's paper showing that the combinatorics of perturbative Feynman diagrams is governed by a commutative Hopf algebra of rooted trees.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~evans/entropy.and.PDE.pdf, Entropy and PDEs by Lawrence Evans. Notes on the thermodynamic foundations of entropy, concavity, Legendre transforms, and entropy solutions of hyperbolic conservation laws.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~evans/math%20170%20notes.pdf, Calculus of Variations. Lawrence Evans' notes on Euler–Lagrange equations, second variations, and minimizers.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~evans/math%20195%20notes.pdf, Optimization Notes. Evans' notes on convex optimization, Lagrange multipliers, and duality.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~vfr/VonNeumann2009.pdf, Von Neumann Algebras by Vaughan Jones. Lecture notes on $W^*$-algebras, the double commutant theorem, traces, and subfactor theory.

https://categorified.net/LieQuantumGroups.pdf, Quantum Groups and Lie Algebras. Notes on quantum deformations $U_q(\mathfrak{g})$, $R$-matrices, and knot invariants.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2601.02290, Dynkin and Coxeter Diagrams. Classification of finite and affine reflection groups via Dynkin diagrams.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/BookAMSULect.pdf, Noncommutative Geometry and Number Theory. Matilde Marcolli's monograph on the Bost–Connes system, modular curves, spectral triples, and arithmetic dynamics.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2410.21244, Homotopy Localizations via Vopenka's Principle. Set-theoretic consistency of Bousfield localizations in higher category theory.

https://benahou.github.io/pdfs/Ben_Introduction_to_Real_Harmonic_Analysis__I__Interpolation_and_Calderon_Zygmund_theory.pdf, Real Harmonic Analysis Primer. Notes on Riesz–Thorin interpolation, maximal functions, and Calderón–Zygmund singular integrals.

https://www.ias.edu/sites/default/files/video/demeter.pdf, Decoupling in Harmonic Analysis. Ciprian Demeter's slides on the Bourgain–Demeter decoupling theorem for hypersurfaces and applications to Strichartz estimates.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~njhu/talks/qbl-2024-07-25/slides/qbl-2024-07-25.pdf, The Brascamp-Lieb Inequality. Slides on multilinear Brascamp–Lieb data, Gaussian optimizers, and quiver representations.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~njhu/talks/deconv-nlw-2023-06-08/slides/deconv-nlw-2023-06-08.pdf, Nonlinear Wave Equations and Deconvolution. Dispersive estimates, scattering operators, and Tauberian deconvolution.

https://www.mathunion.org/fileadmin/IMU/ICM2022/Presentation-slides/49-Larry%20Guth.pdf, Decoupling and PDEs by Larry Guth. ICM plenary address on the geometric method of decoupling, wave packet decompositions, and Vinogradov's mean value theorem.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/files/MeyerMoritaEquivalence-2.pdf, Morita Equivalence of Rings. Categorical properties of Morita equivalences between module categories $\operatorname{Mod}_R \simeq \operatorname{Mod}_S$ via progenerators.

https://www.its.caltech.edu/~matilde/IntroNCGToronto3.pdf, Finite Spectral Triples Primer. Slides on matrix algebras $M_n(\mathbb{C})$, Hilbert modules, Dirac operators, and the classification of discrete noncommutative spaces.

https://www.mit.edu/~9.520/spring12/slides/mathcamp2012-fa-slides.pdf, Functional Analysis Math Camp. MIT slides reviewing Hilbert spaces, Cauchy sequences, Riesz representation, and spectral theorems for symmetric operators.

http://www.tac.mta.ca/tac/volumes/20/15/20-15.pdf, Dualizing Objects and Dualities. Lawvere on dualities generated by a single dualizing object in two categories (e.g., $2 \in \mathbf{Bool}$ and $[0,1] \in \mathbf{Top}$).

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0902.2068, Moduli Spaces of Dirac Operators. Classification of metric spaces of Dirac operators on finite spectral triples.

https://www.math.uchicago.edu/~bloch/graphpoly050928b.pdf, Motives and Graph Polynomials. Spencer Bloch, Hélène Esnault, and Dirk Kreimer on Kirchhoff graph polynomials, Feynman amplitudes, and mixed Tate motives.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0012198v1, Graph Hypersurfaces and Motives. Belkale and Brosnan disproving Kontsevich's conjecture that graph hypersurface point counts over $\mathbb{F}_q$ are polynomial in $q$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1005.4470, Graph Hypersurfaces in the Grothendieck Ring. Proving that graph hypersurfaces generate the Grothendieck ring of varieties $K_0(\mathbf{Var})$.

https://www.math.uchicago.edu/~bloch/graph_sums081005.pdf, Feynman Amplitudes as Graph Sums. Spencer Bloch on algebraic cycles, graph polynomials, and path integral expansions.

https://banica.u-cergy.fr/pdf/ioa.pdf, Introduction to Operator Algebras. Teodor Banica's 80 slides on $C^*$-algebras, von Neumann algebras, Gelfand duality, and non-commutative probability.

https://math.arizona.edu/~cakeller/VertexOperatorAlgebras.pdf, Vertex Operator Algebras. Christoph Keller's 36-page introduction to chiral algebras, conformal blocks, and the Monster vertex algebra.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/07JV, The Snake Lemma in Homological Algebra. Stacks project tag 07JV providing the explicit construction of the connecting homomorphism $\delta: \ker(\gamma) \to \operatorname{coker}(\alpha)$ for $3 \times 3$ commutative diagrams with exact rows.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/00ZX, Abelian Categories. Axiomatic characterization of abelian categories ensuring the validity of the snake lemma and first isomorphism theorem.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/0517, Finitely Presented Modules. Definitions and exact sequences characterizing finitely generated and finitely presented modules over commutative rings.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/download/homology.pdf, Homological Algebra in the Stacks Project. Foundational reference covering additive categories, abelian categories, derived categories, and spectral sequences.

https://www.esaga.uni-due.de/f/marc.levine/publ/Postnikov2.pdf, Motivic Postnikov Towers. Marc Levine's paper on the slice filtration, motivic Postnikov towers, and algebraic $K$-theory.

https://plclark.github.io/PeteLClark/Expositions/integral2015.pdf, Integral Closure and Valuation Rings. Pete L. Clark's 100-page monograph on integral dependence, going-up/going-down theorems, and valuation rings.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/xnotes/CA210.pdf, Primer on Commutative Algebra. J.S. Milne's 65-page primer covering Noetherian rings, localization, primary decomposition, and dimension theory.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Books/ADTnot.pdf, Arithmetic Duality Theorems by J.S. Milne. Comprehensive textbook on local and global Tate duality, Poitou–Tate duality, and Galois cohomology.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Books/DMOS.pdf, Hodge Cycles, Motives, and Shimura Varieties. Deligne, Milne, Ogus, and Shih on absolute Hodge cycles, Tannakian categories, and canonical models.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Books/AA1988a.pdf, Automorphic Forms and Shimura Varieties I. Milne's foundational text on automorphic representations and the geometry of Shimura varieties.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Books/AA1988b.pdf, Automorphic Forms and Shimura Varieties II. Advanced second part covering canonical models and conjugation of Shimura varieties.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Books/ectext6.pdf, Elliptic Curves by J.S. Milne. Comprehensive course text on the geometry and arithmetic of elliptic curves over local and global fields.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Books/iAG2022.pdf, Algebraic Groups by J.S. Milne. 600-page comprehensive textbook on affine group schemes, Lie algebras, and reductive algebraic groups.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Books/tcdraft.pdf, Tannakian Categories by J.S. Milne. Complete textbook draft on rigid tensor categories, fiber functors, and affine group schemes.

https://dspace.mit.edu/bitstream/handle/1721.1/116075.2/WWCM.pdf?sequence=6&isAllowed=y, A Term of Commutative Algebra. MIT textbook on localization, flatness, Cohen–Macaulay rings, and the Nullstellensatz.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/download/algebra.pdf, Commutative Algebra in the Stacks Project. The foundational 1000+ page reference on commutative algebra:
- **Exact Sequences & Finiteness:** Snake lemma connecting homomorphisms, finite presentation criteria, and flatness via Tor vanishing.
- **Localizations & Spectra:** Universal property of localizations $S^{-1}R$, prime avoidance, the Zariski spectrum $\operatorname{Spec}(R)$, and characterization of minimal primes as irreducible components.
- **Dimension & Regularity:** Krull dimension, Cohen–Macaulay rings where homological depth equals Krull dimension, and regular local rings ($\dim R = \dim_{\kappa} \mathfrak{m}/\mathfrak{m}^2$).
- **Differentials & Smoothness:** Modules of Kähler differentials $\Omega_{S/R}^1$, the de Rham complex, formally smooth/unramified/étale ring morphisms, and Henselian local rings.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1405.4527, The Arithmetic Site by Alain Connes. The presheaf topos $\widehat{\mathbb{N}^\times}$ endowed with the structure sheaf of tropical integers $\mathbb{Z}_{\max}$. Proves that points over $\mathbb{R}_{\max}$ recover the adèle class space $X_{\mathbb{Q}} = \mathbb{Q}^\times \backslash \mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{Q}} / \widehat{\mathbb{Z}}^\times$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1407.5782, Points and Topologies over F1. Deligne-type fiber functors and points in characteristic 1 geometries.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1712.07602, Arithmetic Gauge Theory. Minhyong Kim's 29-page paper on arithmetic Chern–Simons theory and non-abelian reciprocity on number rings.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~aaron/livetex/lurie-tamagawa-poincare.pdf, Tamagawa Numbers via Nonabelian Poincare Duality. Jacob Lurie's lecture computing Tamagawa numbers of algebraic groups over function fields via non-abelian Poincaré duality.

https://math.mit.edu/~drew/vantage/KimSlides.pdf, Arithmetic Topology and the Weil Trichotomy. Minhyong Kim's 35 slides on arithmetic topology, mapping class groups, and gauge theories for number rings.

https://people.maths.bris.ac.uk/~matyd/JHC70/Kim.pdf, Diophantine Geometry and Arithmetic Topology. Minhyong Kim's slides on Selmer varieties, unipotent fundamental groups, and rational points.

https://minhyongkim.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/12/madrid2014.pdf, Madrid Lectures on Diophantine Geometry. 113 slides by Minhyong Kim on the arithmetic of curves and non-abelian Chabauty methods.

https://swc-math.github.io/aws/2020/2020KimLecture1Slides.pdf, Selmer Schemes Lecture 1. Minhyong Kim's AWS slides on fundamental groups of schemes, Galois representations, and non-abelian Selmer varieties.

https://swc-math.github.io/aws/2020/2020KimLecture2Slides.pdf, Selmer Schemes Lecture 2. Moduli of unipotent Galois representations and local-to-global restrictions.

https://swc-math.github.io/aws/2020/2020KimLecture3Slides.pdf, Selmer Schemes Lecture 3. Connecting Selmer schemes to the Kim–Chabauty method for rational points on curves.

https://swc-math.github.io/aws/2020/2020KimLecture4Slides.pdf, Selmer Schemes Lecture 4. Explicit computations of Chabauty–Kim loci and arithmetic intersections.

http://cm2vivi2002.free.fr/AC-biblio/AC-biblio79.pdf, Theta-Summable Fredholm Modules. Alain Connes' paper on $\theta$-summable Fredholm modules, entire cyclic cohomology, and Chern characters.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0409520, Noncommutative Geometry and Number Theory. Matilde Marcolli's 100-page lecture notes covering the Bost–Connes system, modular curves, and quantum statistical mechanics.

https://brownmath.com/stfa/read.htm, How to Read Mathematics. Practical advice on reading mathematical texts actively, working out examples, and verifying edge cases.

https://www.math.stonybrook.edu/~fzheng/seminar-f24/notes/week-5-6.pdf, A-Infinity Algebras Dictionary. Dictionary comparing smooth algebraic varieties, tangent vector fields, derivations, and $A_\infty$-algebras.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1506.08376, One-Line Proof of the Weak Nullstellensatz. Direct proof using Zariski's lemma and field extensions over PIDs with infinitely many primes.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1709.06271, Lectures on Infinity-Categories. Vladimir Hinich's 77-page comprehensive course notes covering simplicial sets, quasicategories, Kan extensions, and Cartesian fibrations.

https://math.stackexchange.com/questions/4710666/what-are-the-benefits-of-working-with-smooth-and-admissible-representations, Smooth and Admissible Representations. Discussion on the representation theory of $p$-adic Lie groups, smoothness ($v$ fixed by open subgroups), and admissibility (finite-dimensional fixed-point spaces).

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0803.2587, Gabriel-Zisman Localization of Categories. Accessible introduction to localizing categories along weak equivalences via Ore conditions, formal fractions, and roofs.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2403.03878, Moduli Stacks of Zero-Dimensional Sheaves. Properties of $\operatorname{Coh}^0(X)$, Castelnuovo–Mumford regularity, and moduli of 0-dimensional sheaves with proper support.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~lurie/252xnotes/Lecture20.pdf, Bousfield Localization in Spectra. Jacob Lurie's lecture notes on $E$-acyclic spectra, $E$-local spectra, and the Bousfield localization functor $L_E$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2306.02218, Calculus of Fractions for Quasicategories. Developing formal fractions and localizations directly within the framework of marked simplicial sets and quasicategories.

https://www.math.uwo.ca/faculty/kapulkin/seminars/hottestfiles/Cisinski-2020-04-02-HoTTEST.pdf, Denis-Charles Cisinski on Higher Fibrations. HoTTEST seminar talk on Cartesian fibrations, univalent categories, and model categories.

https://www.oliviacaramello.com/Talks/OliviaCaramello_IntroductoryCourseCentraleSupelecPart1.pdf, Basic Category Theory. Caramello's lecture on categories, functors, natural transformations, limits, and adjunctions.

https://emilyriehl.github.io/files/fibrations.pdf, Categorical Fibrations by Emily Riehl. Overview of Grothendieck fibrations, discrete fibrations, and the Grothendieck construction.

https://www.matrix-inst.org.au/wp_Matrix2016/wp-content/uploads/2017/08/BarwickShah.pdf, Fibrations in Higher Category Theory. 25-page introduction by Barwick and Shah on Cartesian fibrations, coCartesian fibrations, and straightening.

https://webhomes.maths.ed.ac.uk/~cbarwick/papers/fibrations-extra.pdf, Notes on Categorical Fibrations. Clark Barwick's 18-page notes on the homotopy theory of categorical fibrations.

https://www.chapman.edu/scst/conferences-and-events/grothendieck-files/rosolini-slides.pdf, Fibered Categories and Pseudofunctors. Slides on the equivalence between fibered categories over $B$ and pseudofunctors $B^{\text{op}} \to \mathbf{Cat}$.

https://acmbl.github.io/straight_slides.pdf, The Straightening and Unstraightening Theorem. 31 slides detailing Lurie's equivalence between Cartesian fibrations over an $\infty$-category and functors to $\mathbf{Cat}_\infty$.

https://www.oliviacaramello.com/Talks/SlidesTheoriesOfPresheafTypeNew.pdf, Theories of Presheaf Type. Olivia Caramello's slides on classifying toposes of geometric theories and flat functors.

https://www.oliviacaramello.com/Talks/FibredSitesAndExistentialToposesTalk.pdf, Fibered Sites and Existential Toposes. Generalizing locale theory to fibered sites and classifying existential geometric logic.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~vfr/planar.pdf, Planar Algebras by Vaughan Jones. Foundational monograph on shaded planar algebras, subfactor invariants, and knot polynomials.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2109.14594, Reference on Infinity-Categories and SAG. Eugster and Pridham on hidden smoothness, commutative differential graded algebras, and spectral algebraic geometry.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~reb/courses/256A/256A.pdf, Algebraic Geometry Notes by Richard Borcherds. Comprehensive course covering affine/projective schemes, the Nullstellensatz, primary decompositions, blowing up, Serre's twisting sheaf $\mathcal{O}(1)$, Grassmannians, and the Riemann–Roch theorem.

https://agag-gathmann.math.rptu.de/pub/trop-0601322.pdf, Tropical Algebraic Geometry. Andreas Gathmann's 24-page primer on amoebas, the max-plus semiring, tropical curves, and the tropical Bézout theorem.

https://dornsife.usc.edu/aravind-asok/wp-content/uploads/sites/213/2023/10/MATH599.S16.pdf, A1-Homotopy Theory and Algebraic Geometry. Aravind Asok's course notes on Morel–Voevodsky $\mathbb{A}^1$-homotopy theory, vector bundles, and motivic spheres.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2009.10602, Finite Groups with Low-Dimensional Modules. 2-page proof that if all simple $FG$-modules have dimension $< p$, then $G$ has a normal Sylow $p$-subgroup.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1909.03709, Short Presentations of Finite Simple Groups. Survey on $(2,3)$-generation and short presentations for finite simple groups.

https://dspace.mit.edu/bitstream/handle/1721.1/151192/18-218-spring-2019/contents/lecture-notes/MIT18_218S19_full_notes.pdf, Probabilistic Methods in Combinatorics. Andrew Lin's 124-page MIT lecture notes covering second moment methods, Chernoff bounds, the Lovász Local Lemma, and graphons.

https://andrewodesky.com/res/sieves.pdf, Sieve Methods in Arithmetic Statistics. Notes on Brun's and Selberg's sieves applied to point counts and arithmetic statistics.

https://iris.uniroma1.it/retrieve/e383531a-c364-15e8-e053-a505fe0a3de9/DHA.pdf, Discrete Harmonic Analysis Monograph. 595-page treatise on discrete singular integrals, radon transforms, and additive combinatorics.

https://eugeniomaths.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/03/restricting-irreducible-characters-to-sylow-p-subgroups.pdf, Restricting Irreducible Characters to Sylow Subgroups. Expository notes on character restrictions, Clifford's theorem, and $p$-local representation theory.

https://www.iazd.uni-hannover.de/fileadmin/iazd/sambale/pdfs/2gensylow.pdf, Groups with 2-Generated Sylow Subgroups. Saturated fusion systems on 2-generated $p$-groups.

https://legacy-www.math.harvard.edu/theses/senior/mathew/mathew.pdf, Galois Groups in Stable Homotopy Theory. Akhil Mathew's senior thesis on Galois extensions of commutative ring spectra, topological modular forms, and chromatic homotopy.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/9906167, Intuitive Introduction to Conformal Field Theory. Monograph on vertex operator algebras, the Virasoro algebra, and conformal blocks.

https://t2r2.star.titech.ac.jp/rrws/file/CTT100830984/ATD100000413/, Thesis on q-Whittaker Polynomials. Research thesis on Macdonald polynomials at $t=0$, quantum integrable systems, and Toda lattices.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1708.01175, The 27 Lines via Motivic Homotopy Theory. Kass and Wickelgren's paper computing the 27 lines on a smooth cubic surface over general fields via the $\mathbb{A}^1$-Euler class in the Grothendieck–Witt ring $\operatorname{GW}(k)$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2204.01162, Right-Angled Artin Groups and CAT(0) Spaces. Studies amenability, hyperbolic geometry, and subgroup structures of RAAGs.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1910.10407, The Spectral Point of View in Physics. Alain Connes on operator algebras, noncommutative standard models, and the emergence of time.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1207.5449, Extended Chern-Simons Theories. Freed and Teleman on fully extended higher cup product Chern–Simons theories.

https://www.ias.edu/sites/default/files/pcmi-2024-07-04_0.pdf, Massey Products in Homotopy Theory. 22-page notes on higher Massey products in differential graded algebras and singular cohomology.

https://dec41.user.srcf.net/exp/freed-hopkins/freed-hopkins.pdf, Chern-Weil Forms and Abstract Homotopy Theory. 10-page exposition by Freed and Hopkins on characteristic classes as natural transformations in differential cohomology.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1510.07270, Exponential Complexes and Periods. Javier Fresán on exponential motives, period integrals, and comparison isomorphisms.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1705.09945, Exact Calculations in Chern-Simons Theory. Non-perturbative computations of path integrals and quantum invariants of 3-manifolds.

https://academicweb.nd.edu/~pmnev/f19/Chern-Weil.pdf, Connections, Curvatures, and Characteristic Classes. Pavel Mnev's lecture notes on Chern–Weil theory on principal bundles.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math-ph/0511034, Tomita-Takesaki Theory and Physics. Stephen Summers' comprehensive survey on modular theory, KMS equilibrium states, and algebraic quantum field theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1803.04993, Entanglement Properties in Quantum Field Theory. Edward Witten's 100-page lecture notes on Type III factors, modular theory, and entanglement entropy in QFT.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1902.05306, The Spectral Action Principle Survey. Chamseddine and Connes reviewing the spectral action principle on noncommutative manifolds.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2010.12534, Diagram Chasing Primer. Concise guide to diagram chasing lemmas (Snake lemma, 5-lemma, 9-lemma) in abelian categories.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2311.01790, First-Order Diagram Chasing. Formalizing diagram chasing as a first-order logic theory over quivers.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1704.01863, The Universal Isomorphism Theorem. Axiomatizing group isomorphism theorems through Galois connections on subgroup lattices.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1008.0129, Renormalization in Quantum Field Theory by Borcherds. Richard Borcherds' formulation of perturbative QFT, Feynman propagators, and the Epstein–Glaser renormalization method using sheaves on spacetime manifolds.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math-ph/0204014, Borcherds' Quantum Field Theory Notes. Comprehensive notes on classical field theories, Euler–Lagrange equations, Noether currents, and path integrals.

https://web.math.princeton.edu/~chenyang/Kstabilitybook.pdf, K-Stability of Algebraic Varieties. Chenyang Xu's monograph on K-stability, Fano varieties, and the existence of Kähler–Einstein metrics.

https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/000187087190020X, Symplectic Geometry and Classical Mechanics. Kostant's foundational paper on geometric quantization and coadjoint orbits.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.12520, Strong Convergence in Random Matrix Theory. Survey on operator-valued free probability, Haagerup inequalities, and asymptotic spectral distributions.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~katrin/teach/jhol/, Pseudo-Holomorphic Curves Course. Katrin Wehrheim's course materials on $J$-holomorphic curves and Floer homology.

https://math.berkeley.edu/~katrin/teach/quilts/, Pseudoholomorphic Quilts. Wehrheim's notes on quilted Floer homology and Lagrangian correspondences.

https://people.math.ethz.ch/~salamon/PREPRINTS/floer.pdf, Lectures on Floer Homology. Dietmar Salamon's classic lecture notes on symplectic action functionals, gradient flows, and Floer homology.

http://igm.univ-mlv.fr/~fpsac/FPSAC04/ARTICLES/Adin1.pdf, Axiomatic Theory of Coxeter Groups and Hecke Algebras. Representation theory of Coxeter systems via abstract Young pairs and Cayley graphs.

https://nsalter.science.nd.edu/expository-notes/harvard41520.pdf, Strata of Moduli Spaces of Differentials. Nick Salter's notes on moduli of abelian differentials and mapping class groups.

https://central.bac-lac.canada.ca/.item?id=MR49680&op=pdf&app=Library&oclc_number=697932072, The McKay Correspondence Thesis. Master's thesis detailing the bijection between finite subgroups of $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ and simply laced Dynkin diagrams ($A_n, D_n, E_6, E_7, E_8$).

https://mathcs.holycross.edu/~little/MathInTheMountains/MMTTTorics1.pdf, Affine Tropicalization. 50 slides by John Little on valuation theory, tropical varieties, and polyhedral complexes.

https://mathcs.holycross.edu/~little/MathInTheMountains/MMTTTorics2.pdf, Fans, Orbits, and Divisors in Toric Geometry. Orbit-cone correspondences, invariant Cartier divisors, and intersection theory on toric varieties.

https://timothy-magee.github.io/assets/notes/ToricVarieties-Lecture1.pdf, Toric Varieties Course. Graduate lecture slides on toric affine varieties and coordinate rings.

https://mathcs.holycross.edu/~little/MathInTheMountains/MMTTModelLatest.pdf, Moment Maps and Toric Varieties. Symplectic reduction, Delzant polytopes, and moment maps for Hamiltonian torus actions.

https://math.mit.edu/~dav/mckay.pdf, The McKay Correspondence by David Vogan. Clean proof characterizing finite subgroups of $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ via harmonic labeled graphs and ADE classification.

http://www.liegroups.org/papers/, Atlas of Lie Groups Papers. Archive of computational and theoretical research on representations of reductive Lie groups.

https://math.mit.edu/~dav/e8wpiHOedit.pdf, The Character Table of E8 by David Vogan. Expository account of computing the character table and irreducible representations of the split real Lie group $E_8$.

https://www.math.uni-bielefeld.de/~sek/kau/bgp.pdf, The Bernstein-Gelfand-Ponomarev Theorem. Classic paper establishing Gabriel's theorem: a quiver has finitely many indecomposable representations if and only if its underlying graph is an ADE Dynkin diagram.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1309.5055, Torsion in Schubert Varieties. Geordie Williamson on torsion in the intersection cohomology of Schubert varieties and counterexamples to the Lusztig conjecture.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1403.1650, The Kazhdan-Lusztig Conjectures. Introduction to flag manifolds $G/B$, perverse sheaves, Soergel bimodules, and Kazhdan–Lusztig polynomials.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1510.01556, The p-Canonical Basis and Soergel Bimodules. Jensen and Williamson on $p$-canonical bases of Hecke algebras and modular representation theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2111.15161, Coxeter Groups and Bruhat Graphs. 47-page introduction to Coxeter groups, Bruhat intervals, and Kazhdan–Lusztig combinatorics.

https://www2.math.upenn.edu/~harbater/luxtalks.pdf, Formal Patching in Algebra. David Harbater's notes on formal patching, rigid analytic spaces, and Galois realizations over arithmetic fields.

https://www2.math.upenn.edu/~harbater/lggalcohom.pdf, Local-Global Principles in Galois Cohomology. Higher-dimensional local-global principles for Brauer groups and quadratic forms over function fields.

https://www.konradwaldorf.de/docs/pittsburgh.pdf, Gerbes, Loop Spaces, and Chern-Simons Theory. Konrad Waldorf's lecture notes on bundle gerbes with connections, surface holonomies, and the string group.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~galashin/slides/qtcat_Stanford.pdf, Cluster Algebras and Quiver Mutations. Pavel Galashin's slides on cluster varieties, positive Grassmannians, and scattering amplitudes.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~galashin/slides/ipam_w2_move_reduced.pdf, Geometry of the Amplituhedron. Slides on Grassmannian geometry, canonical differential forms, and the physics of the amplituhedron.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~pak/lectures/Slides/OPAC-talk-UMN-2022-short.pdf, Combinatorial Interpretations and Complexity. Igor Pak on computational complexity classes and combinatorial counting formulas.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~pak/lectures/Slides/UIUC-talk-2022-short.pdf, Inequalities in Algebraic Combinatorics. Igor Pak on log-concavity, unimodality, and Hook length formulas.

https://www.lpthe.jussieu.fr/~pzinn/summary.pdf, Integrability and Symmetric Functions. Paul Zinn-Justin on quantum integrability, matrix models, and symmetric Grothendieck polynomials.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~pak/lectures/Slides/Pak-CT-OSU-talk-2020-short.pdf, Contingency Tables and Posets. Slides on counting integer contingency tables and linear extensions of posets.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~pak/lectures/Slides/Lake-Arrowhead-talk-LA-expanders-short.pdf, Linear Algebra in Combinatorics. Combinatorial bounds derived from spectral gaps of expander graphs.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~pak/lectures/Slides/CJCS-talk-LE-Stanley.pdf, Stanley's Inequalities for Posets. Linear extensions of partially ordered sets and the Alexandrov–Fenchel inequality.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~pak/lectures/Slides/Banff-posets-talk-2021-short.pdf, Random Linear Extensions. Generating random linear extensions of posets via Markov chain Monte Carlo.

https://www.math.ucla.edu/~pak/lectures/Slides/MIP%20--%20talk%20--%20May%2024%20--%202023%20--%20short.pdf, Integer Points in Polytopes. Computational complexity of counting lattice points in convex polytopes.

https://www.maths.usyd.edu.au/u/geordie/perverse_course/lectures.pdf, Lectures on Perverse Sheaves. Geordie Williamson's geometric course notes covering Whitney stratifications, local systems, intersection cohomology, and the Decomposition Theorem of Beilinson, Bernstein, Deligne, and Gabber (BBDG).

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1705.02129, Monodromy Representations of Elliptic Curves. Monodromy actions of $\pi_1(B)$ on the cohomology of elliptic surface fibers and the symplectic group $\operatorname{Sp}_{2g}(\mathbb{Z})$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1403.5800, Perverse Sheaves on Hyperplane Arrangements. Cohomology of local systems on complements of complex hyperplane arrangements.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1411.2772, Perverse Schobers. Kapranov and Schechtman on perverse schobers as categorified perverse sheaves and spherical functors.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2512.22718, Perverse Sheaves and Fourier Transforms. Categorical Fourier–Sato transforms on perverse sheaves and character sheaves.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1506.03642, Perverse Sheaves on Algebraic Varieties. Foundational survey of perverse sheaves, intersection cohomology, and $t$-structures.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1801.00896, Parity Sheaves by Juteau, Mautner, and Williamson. Foundational paper constructing parity sheaves over general coefficient rings, resolving modular representation conjectures where the Decomposition Theorem fails.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1610.06261, Algebraic Groups and Constructible Sheaves. Survey by Geordie Williamson linking representations of reductive algebraic groups over $\mathbb{F}_p$ to parity sheaves on the affine Grassmannian.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1405.3479, Schubert Stratifications. 13-page primer on Schubert cells, Bruhat order, and intersection cohomology of flag varieties.

https://mathdoctorbob.org/20250312_Borel_Weil_Bott_v5.pdf, The Borel-Weil-Bott Theorem. 49 slides on inducing irreducible representations of compact Lie groups from holomorphic line bundles over flag varieties $G/B$.

https://archive.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/id/eprint/2346/1/preprint_1992_50.pdf, Yuri Manin's Lectures on Zeta Functions. Monograph on the geometry of $\operatorname{Spec} \mathbb{Z}$, regularized determinants, and motives.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2512.03338, Locally Compact Groups and Abelian Categorification. Exact categories and homological algebra for locally compact abelian groups.

https://conservancy.umn.edu/server/api/core/bitstreams/975fc809-0ccc-4c01-9751-31903c891a4e/content, Meditations on Eisenstein Series. Paul Garrett's notes on the spectral decomposition, intertwining operators, and meromorphic continuation of Eisenstein series.

https://www.cl.cam.ac.uk/teaching/1011/RLFA/LectureNotes.pdf, Regular Languages and Finite Automata. Cambridge course notes on regular expressions, deterministic finite automata (DFA), and Kleene's theorem.

https://www.pnas.org/doi/pdf/10.1073/pnas.2500940122, Singularity Formation in 3D Euler Equations. Self-similar finite-time blow-up solutions for the 3D incompressible Euler equations.

https://epubs.siam.org/doi/full/10.1137/23M1580395, Numerical Analysis of Euler Blow-Ups. High-precision numerical methods verifying finite-time singularity formation in fluid dynamics.

https://www.andrew.cmu.edu/user/awodey/students/jackson.pdf, Measure Theory in Topos Theory. Master's thesis developing point-free measure theory and integrals in elementary topoi.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1707.07663, Microlocal Sheaves and Wrapped Fukaya Categories. Nadler and Shende on computing wrapped Fukaya categories of Weinstein manifolds via constructible sheaves on the Lagrangian skeleton.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2312.08123, Microlocal Analysis and the Gelfand Problem. Survey on pseudo-differential operators, wave front sets, and the Gelfand spectral problem.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1507.08735, Combinatorial Landau-Ginzburg Models. 11-page notes on the mirror symmetry of Landau–Ginzburg models and matrix factorizations.

https://scispace.com/pdf/microlocal-sheaves-and-mirror-symmetry-itf1xmoqwj.pdf, Microlocal Sheaves and Mirror Symmetry. Primer on the Nadler–Zaslow correspondence matching the Fukaya category $\operatorname{Fuk}(T^*M)$ with $\mathcal{D}^b(\operatorname{Shv}(M))$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1712.03708, p-Adic Geometry and GL(2) Langlands. Peter Scholze's survey on the $p$-adic geometry of modular curves, $p$-adic Galois representations, and local Langlands for $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Q}_p)$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2409.04677, Foundations of Relative Langlands Duality. 400-page comprehensive treatise by Ben-Zvi, Sakellaridis, and Venkatesh on Hamiltonian spaces, periods, and relative Langlands.

https://www.mathematik.uni-muenchen.de/~land/K-theory.pdf, Markus Land's Notes on Algebraic K-Theory. 100-page notes on Quillen $K$-theory, Waldhausen categories, and chromatic localization.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2102.13459, Geometric Satake on the Fargues-Fontaine Curve. Fargues and Scholze on the Geometric Satake Equivalence on the $B_{\text{dR}}$-affine Grassmannian and the local Langlands correspondence.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1704.07819, Exceptional Lie Algebra G2. Structural notes on the 14-dimensional exceptional Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}_2$, its root system, and octonionic derivations.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1603.05606, Cartan-Weyl Basis for G2. Explicit matrix realization and commutation relations for the $\mathfrak{g}_2$ Cartan–Weyl basis.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1611.04070, Maximal Subalgebras of G2. Classification of maximal Lie subalgebras of the exceptional algebra $\mathfrak{g}_2$.

https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/18-755-lie-groups-and-lie-algebras-ii-spring-2024/mit18_755_s24_lec_full.pdf, Lie Groups and Lie Algebras II by Pavel Etingof. MIT course notes covering root systems, Weyl groups, highest weight representations, and the Weyl character formula.

https://www.math.tecnico.ulisboa.pt/~jnatar/MAGEF-23/trabalhos/Bernardo.pdf, SU(3) in Particle Physics. 5-page introduction to Gell-Mann matrices, the quark model, and $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ flavor symmetry.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2512.03648, Condensed Group Cohomology. Foundations of group cohomology for locally compact groups developed within condensed mathematics.

https://www.math.ens.psl.eu/~benoist/refs/Eisenbud.pdf, Commutative Algebra with a View Toward Algebraic Geometry. David Eisenbud's classic 800-page textbook on Gröbner bases, free resolutions, Hilbert functions, and dimension theory.

https://webhomes.maths.ed.ac.uk/~v1ranick/papers/eisenbudharris.pdf, The Geometry of Schemes. Eisenbud and Harris's introductory textbook covering the language of schemes, morphisms, glued schemes, and projective geometry.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1904.00179, Computations of Motivic Periods. Brown and Dupont on computing motivic periods, multiple zeta values, and mixed Tate motives.

https://math.stanford.edu/~conrad/papers/luminysga3smf.pdf, Worked Examples of Reductive Groups. Brian Conrad's Luminy notes detailing explicit constructions of split and non-split reductive group schemes.

https://pi.math.cornell.edu/~allenk/courses/10spring/7670/notes.pdf, Allen Knutson's Sheaf Cohomology Notes. Lecture notes on Grothendieck groups $K_0(X)$, tangent sheaves, and Serre duality on $\mathbb{P}^1$.

https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/01XS, Cohomology of Projective Schemes. Stacks project tag 01XS computing $H^q(\mathbb{P}_A^n, \mathcal{O}(d))$ and the Euler characteristic of twisting sheaves.

https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/18-726-algebraic-geometry-spring-2009/da2b637d79c2c9d13d208a04fd47b21c_MIT18_726s09_lec19_cohomproj.pdf, Serre's Computation of Cohomology on Pn. MIT lecture notes detailing Serre's explicit computation of $H^i(\mathbb{P}^n, \mathcal{O}(m))$ via Čech cohomology.

https://math.uchicago.edu/~may/REU2013/REUPapers/Fung.pdf, Computations in Serre Duality. REU paper verifying Serre duality pairings and dualizing sheaves on smooth projective varieties.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2102.02245, Introduction to Siegel Modular Forms. Geometric primer on vector-valued Siegel modular forms, Satake compactifications, and moduli stacks of abelian varieties $\mathcal{A}_g$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1604.02654, Cohomological Aspects of Siegel Modular Forms. 20-page survey on the cohomology of Siegel modular varieties and automorphic vector bundles.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1907.08783, Foundations of Siegel Modular Forms. Comprehensive lecture notes covering the symplectic group $\operatorname{Sp}_{2g}(\mathbb{R})$, Siegel upper half-space $\mathbb{H}_g$, and Hecke operators.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2307.04115, Motivic Actions on Siegel Modular Forms. Action of motivic Galois groups on the cohomology of Siegel modular threefolds.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2401.01446, David Vogan on Topological Vector Spaces. Foundational notes on locally convex Fréchet spaces and smooth vectors in representation theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1711.08169, Representation Theory of PSL(2, R). Basis of the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb{R})$, standard triplets $(e, h, f)$, and unitary representation classes.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1512.08296, Tilting Modules for Reductive Algebraic Groups. New geometric approach to tilting modules in positive characteristic using $\mathcal{D}$-modules and affine Grassmannians.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0904.1247, Topological Field Theory and Representation Theory. Lurie's notes connecting derived algebraic geometry, TFTs, and the representation theory of reductive groups.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1212.0791, Soergel Bimodules and Hecke Categories. Elias and Williamson's paper establishing the diagrammatic presentation of the Hecke category and the Kazhdan–Lusztig conjecture.

https://masaooi.github.io/LAG.pdf, Course on Algebraic Groups. Masao Oi's lecture notes covering linear algebraic groups, Jordan decompositions, and Borel subgroups.

https://people.csail.mit.edu/joonwonc/papers/bsthesis-math.pdf, Category Theory and Universal Constructions. Pushouts as fibered sums and pullbacks as fibered products in categorical logic.

https://webusers.imj-prg.fr/~pierre.schapira/LectNotes/CatShv.pdf, Categories and Sheaves by Pierre Schapira. 200+ page foundational text covering abelian categories, sheaves, derived functors, and Grothendieck topologies.

https://chriskuo0.github.io/homepage/Thesis.pdf, Microlocal Sheaves and Symplectic Geometry. PhD thesis on the micro-support of constructible sheaves and wrapped Fukaya categories.

https://par.nsf.gov/servlets/purl/10600030, Classification of Sheaf Functors via Integral Transforms. Theorem classifying all colimit-preserving functors between categories of constructible sheaves with isotropic support as integral transforms.

https://irma.math.unistra.fr/~liu/Liegpext.pdf, Group Extensions in Complex Geometry. Classification of complex Lie group extensions, non-abelian cohomology, and complex tori.

https://masaooi.github.io/BT.pdf, Bruhat-Tits Theory for p-Adic Groups. Notes on affine buildings, parahoric subgroups, and the structure of $p$-adic reductive groups.

https://people.mpim-bonn.mpg.de/zagier/files-restricted/doi/10.1007/978-3-540-74119-0/fulltext.pdf, Congruences between Siegel and Elliptic Modular Forms. Günter Harder's appendix on automorphic congruences and Galois representations.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1511.02888, Hodge Theory of Matroids. Adiprasito, Huh, and Katz's paper constructing the Chow ring $\underline{A}^*(M) = S_M / (I_M + J_M)$ of a matroid, proving the hard Lefschetz theorem and the Hodge–Riemann relations, and establishing the log-concavity of characteristic polynomial coefficients.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2103.00048, Categorification of sl(2)-Actions. 12-page introduction to categorified $\mathfrak{sl}_2$-actions on derived categories of modules.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1501.06731, Introduction to Derived Categories. 17-page primer on homotopy categories $\operatorname{Ho}(\mathbf{Ch})$, derived categories $\mathcal{D}(\mathcal{A})$, and Verdier localizations.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2504.07502, Modern Survey of the Langlands Program. Comprehensive review of the classical, $p$-adic, and geometric Langlands correspondences.

https://math.uchicago.edu/~may/REU2021/REUPapers/Niemiro.pdf, Sheaf Cohomology via Derived Functors. 12-page REU paper detailing the construction of sheaf cohomology $H^i(X, \mathcal{F}) = R^i\Gamma(X, \mathcal{F})$ via injective resolutions in abelian categories of $\mathcal{O}_X$-modules.

https://www.mat.uniroma2.it/~schoof/historyweibel.pdf, History of Homological Algebra. Charles Weibel's survey tracing homological algebra from Cartan and Eilenberg to Grothendieck and derived categories.

https://www.math.stonybrook.edu/~mde/papers/bams.pdf, The Decomposition Theorem and Hard Lefschetz. De Cataldo and Migliorini's BAMS survey on the Beilinson–Bernstein–Deligne–Gabber (BBDG) Decomposition Theorem for perverse sheaves and algebraic maps.

https://people.math.harvard.edu/~achenjang/assets/pdf/Etale_Coh___Weil_Notes.pdf, Etale Cohomology and the Weil Conjectures. 129-page lecture notes covering étale sites, proper base change, purity, and the proof of the Weil conjectures following Daniel Litt.

https://websites.umich.edu/~viktorb/etale_summer2020/Notes/cohomology-of-number-fields.pdf, Cohomology of Number Fields. 4-page notes on the étale cohomology of $\operatorname{Spec} \mathcal{O}_K$ and Artin–Verdier duality.

https://dfoiler.github.io/handouts/one-offs/etale.pdf, Etale Cohomology in 24 Slides. Rapid slide introduction to étale morphisms, étale sites, and the Weil conjectures.

https://math.mit.edu/juvitop/pastseminars/notes_2021_Spring/etale_thomason.pdf, Crash Course in Etale Cohomology. 13 slides on étale topology, constructible sheaves, and comparison with topological cohomology.

https://www.math.columbia.edu/~dejong/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2015/04/EtaleCohomology.pdf, Johan de Jong's Etale Cohomology Notes. Foundational Columbia lecture notes covering étale morphisms, proper base change, and cycle classes.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Documents/DeligneArcata.pdf, Pierre Deligne's Arcata Notes on Etale Cohomology. SGA $4\frac{1}{2}$ foundational notes covering sieves, Grothendieck topologies, descent theory, and $\ell$-adic cohomology.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/CourseNotes/LEC.pdf, J.S. Milne's Lectures on Etale Cohomology. 200+ page textbook covering étale morphisms, sheaves on étale sites, proper base change, the smooth specialization theorem, Poincaré duality, and the Weil conjectures.

https://web.ma.utexas.edu/users/slaoui/notes/Sheaf_Cohomology_3.pdf, Primer on Sheaf Cohomology. Notes on sheaves on smooth manifolds, constant sheaves, and the transition to the six-functor formalism.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Documents/fac.pdf, English Translation of Serre's FAC. Milne's translation of Faisceaux Algébriques Cohérents developing coherent sheaves on Zariski varieties.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Documents/Artin1967.pdf, Autoduality of the Jacobian. Michael Artin's 1967 paper establishing the autoduality $\operatorname{Jac}(C) \cong \operatorname{Jac}(C)^\vee$ of the Jacobian of a curve.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Documents/Deligne82.pdf, Hodge Cycles on Abelian Varieties. Deligne's foundational paper proving that all Hodge cycles on abelian varieties are absolute Hodge.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Documents/Deligne1989.pdf, Fundamental Group of the Projective Line Minus Three Points. Pierre Deligne's landmark paper on the motivic fundamental group of $\mathbb{P}^1 \setminus \{0,1,\infty\}$, mixed Tate motives, and the Ihara conjecture.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Documents/DeligneSV.pdf, Deligne on Shimura Varieties. Classic paper defining canonical models and modern Shimura data $(G, X)$.

https://www.jmilne.org/math/Documents/DeligneWeilI.pdf, Deligne's Proof of the Weil Conjectures (Weil I). Pierre Deligne's 1974 paper proving the Riemann hypothesis for algebraic varieties over finite fields using $\ell$-adic cohomology and the Rankin–Selberg method.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1808.02923, Conway's Knot is Not Slice. Lisa Piccirillo's landmark 4-page paper proving that Conway's knot is not smoothly slice using Rasmussen's $s$-invariant and dualizable trace patterns.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1902.04050, Concordance and Knot Floer Homology. Survey on smooth knot concordance and link Floer homology.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1705.09989, The Lightbulb Lemma in 4-Manifold Topology. David Gabai's paper on the 4D lightbulb theorem and ambient isotopies of immersed 2-spheres in 4-manifolds.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1604.03466, Bordered Heegaard Floer Homology for Torus Boundaries. Geometric interpretation of bordered $\widehat{HF}$ invariants for 3-manifolds with boundary.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2302.09016, Fusion Systems in Representation Theory. Saturated fusion systems in the modular representation theory of finite groups.

https://hal.science/hal-03840884v1/document, Simplicity of Saturated Fusion Systems. Classification and structure of simple saturated fusion systems on finite $p$-groups.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2507.13471, Prismatic Steenrod Operations and Brauer Duality. Formulating mod-$p$ Steenrod operations on prismatic cohomology and arithmetic duality for Brauer groups.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1007.2925, Short Course on Infinity-Categories. Moritz Groth's 77-page introductory course on quasicategories, Kan complexes, limits, and colimits.

https://www.simonsfoundation.org/video/jacob-lurie-prismatic-stable-homotopy-theory/, Jacob Lurie on Prismatic Stable Homotopy. Lecture exploring the synthesis of prismatic cohomology and chromatic stable homotopy theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2104.07090, Ring Groupoids by Vladimir Drinfeld. Expository paper defining 1-connective ring groupoids and their role in formal deformation theory.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1809.01957, Fusion Systems on Maximal Class 3-Groups. Classification of saturated fusion systems on groups of maximal class and rank 2.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0412512, Grothendieck Topologies, Fibered Categories, and Descent. Angelo Vistoli's foundational notes on Grothendieck topologies, fibered categories, the 2-Yoneda lemma, and descent theory for stacks.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1211.6700, Trees of Saturated Fusion Systems. Amalgams and tree structures for saturated fusion systems.

https://case.edu/artsci/math/mwmeckes/matrix-analysis.pdf, Matrix Analysis by Mark Meckes. 150-page course notes on unitary operators, the spectral theorem, Rayleigh quotients, and matrix norms.

https://math.bme.hu/~petz/matrixPD.pdf, Positive Definite Matrices. Dénes Petz's text on matrix inequalities, Loewner order, and operator convexity.

https://djalil.chafai.net/docs/sing.pdf, Extreme Singular Values of Random Matrices. Notes on non-asymptotic bounds for the smallest and largest singular values of random matrices.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2203.07082, Survey on Modern Representation Theory. Geordie Williamson's survey covering:
- **GL(n) as Quantum S(n):** Tits' idea $S_n = \mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{F}_1)$, Bruhat decompositions, and quantum deformations to Iwahori–Hecke algebras.
- **Categorification:** Passing from groups to categories, Soergel bimodules, and diagrammatic 2-Kac–Moody algebras.
- **Geometric Satake:** The equivalence $\operatorname{Perv}_{G(\mathcal{O})}(\operatorname{Gr}_G) \cong \operatorname{Rep}(G^\vee)$ on the affine Grassmannian.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1702.00039, Introduction to Coxeter Groups. Comprehensive survey on Coxeter systems, root systems, Bruhat orders, and reflection representations.

https://www.claymath.org/library/cw/arthur/pdf/62.pdf, James Arthur on the Trace Formula. Introductory survey on the Arthur–Selberg trace formula and its applications to the Langlands program.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2103.02329, Primer on Geometric Langlands. 11-page concise introduction to the geometric Langlands correspondence, the Hitchin system, and $\mathcal{D}$-modules on $\operatorname{Bun}_G$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1611.02210, Introduction to Skew Howe Duality. Geometric and representation-theoretic formulations of $(\mathrm{GL}_n, \mathrm{GL}_m)$ skew Howe duality.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1606.08523, Betti Geometric Langlands. Ben-Zvi and Nadler on the Betti geometric Langlands correspondence for Riemann surfaces.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0803.4121, Foundations of Categorification. Mikhail Khovanov, Aaron Lauda, and collaborators on 2-categories, categorified quantum groups, and diagrammatic calculus.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2405.04329, Algebraic K-Theory of Truncated Polynomials. Computations of $K_*(\mathbb{Z}/p^n)$ and topological cyclic homology.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0408337, Homotopy Categories of DG Categories. Bernhard Keller and Georges Maltsiniotis on model structures on the category of small differential graded categories.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/0704.4003, Weight Structures versus t-Structures. Mikhail Bondarko's paper introducing weight structures on triangulated categories and comparing them to $t$-structures.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1507.08697, Six Functors on Enriched Categories. Constructing the six operations on enriched stable $\infty$-categories.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0608228, Stable Infinity-Categories by Jacob Lurie (DAG I). Foundational 73-page text establishing stable $\infty$-categories, fiber/cofiber sequences, and spectrum enrichment.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2411.09115, Introduction to Spectral Sequences. Benjamin Antieau's lecture notes covering the Serre, Adams, and descent spectral sequences.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2110.10212, Six Operations for Sheaves on Manifolds. Systematic formulation of the six functors on smooth manifolds via derived categories of sheaves.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1806.06129, Fibrations in Category Theory. Monograph on Grothendieck fibrations, comma categories $(f/g)$, and slice categories.

https://ncatlab.org/nlab/show/cocycle, Cocycle Spaces and Non-Abelian Cohomology. Defining cocycle spaces as $\infty$-groupoids whose objects are cocycles and edges are gauge transformations, with $\pi_0$ giving the cohomology set.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2305.01474, Comonads for Grothendieck Fibrations. Categorical comonads and the classification of Grothendieck fibrations.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1809.00727, The Monoidal Grothendieck Construction. Moeller and Vasilakopoulou on the monoidal Grothendieck construction for Cartesian fibrations.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1801.02927, Jean Benabou on Fibered Categories. Foundational 1973 notes on fibered categories, internal categories, and descent.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2504.09520, Categorical Nerves and Infinity-Categories. Explicit simplicial nerve constructions and quasicategories.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/1810.12250, Chromatic Homotopy Theory is Algebraic. Franke's algebraic models for chromatic homotopy categories at height $n$.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2111.10837, Chromatic Redshift Computations. Explicit calculations demonstrating chromatic height shifts in algebraic $K$-theory.

https://www.mn.uio.no/math/personer/vit/rognes/papers/msri.pdf, John Rognes on Chromatic Redshift. MSRI slides on the chromatic redshift conjecture for structured ring spectra.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2108.13542, Introduction to Topological Modular Forms. 50-page survey on the spectrum $\operatorname{tmf}$, modular curves, and Witten genus invariants.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2207.09929, Chromatic Nullstellensatz. Burklund, Hahn, and Levy on the chromatic Nullstellensatz and Lubin–Tate spectra.

https://arxiv.org/pdf/2310.17459, Counterexamples in Algebraic K-Theory. Analysis of counterexamples to descent and excision in non-connective algebraic $K$-theory.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Weil/Kloosterman.pdf, Kloosterman Sums and Deligne's Weil II. Aleksander Shmakov's notes on $\ell$-adic sheaves and exponential sum bounds.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Weil/Lefschetz.pdf, Lefschetz Pencils and the Radon Transform. Notes on Picard–Lefschetz theory and the Brylinski–Radon transform on étale cohomology.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Weil/perverse.pdf, l-Adic Perverse Sheaves. Shmakov's notes on perverse $\ell$-adic sheaves and intersection cohomology.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Weil/Weil_II.pdf, Monodromy and Purity in Weil II. Detailed notes on Deligne's Weil II, weight filtrations, and the hard Lefschetz theorem.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Iwasawa.pdf, Introduction to Iwasawa Theory. Notes on $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extensions, characteristic ideals, and the Main Conjecture.

https://shmakov.us/docs/LubinTate.pdf, Lubin-Tate Formal Groups. Notes on formal group laws, Lubin–Tate extensions, and local class field theory.

https://shmakov.us/docs/dilog.pdf, The Bloch-Wigner Dilogarithm. Notes on the single-valued Bloch–Wigner dilogarithm $D(z) = \operatorname{Im}(\operatorname{Li}_2(z)) + \arg(1-z)\log|z|$ and hyperbolic volumes.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Slides/ArithmeticStatistics.pdf, Arithmetic Statistics Slides. Slides on Cohen–Lenstra heuristics, moments of class groups, and elliptic curve ranks.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Slides/CanonicalPaths.pdf, Single-Valued Iterated Integrals. Slides on Chen iterated integrals and single-valued polylogarithms on Riemann surfaces.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Slides/LightningSlides.pdf, Moduli of Abelian Varieties and Local Systems. Cohomology of local systems on $\mathcal{A}_g$ and Siegel modular forms.

https://public.websites.umich.edu/~bhattb/almost_purity_2011/Faltings_-_p-adic-Hodge-theory.pdf, Faltings' p-Adic Hodge Theory. Gerd Faltings' paper establishing the almost purity theorem and comparison isomorphisms for $p$-adic Galois representations.

https://bheuer.github.io/moduli-stacks-pnaHT.pdf, Non-Abelian p-Adic Hodge Theory. Ben Heuer's 52-page notes on moduli stacks of $v$-bundles and non-abelian $p$-adic Hodge theory on curves.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Slides/Moduli.pdf, Moduli of Curves and Computations. Detailed slide tutorial on Deligne–Mumford moduli spaces $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}$, stable curves, nodality criteria, and explicit computations for $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{0,3} \cong \{*\}$.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Slides/Zagier.pdf, Zagier's Conjecture and Hyperbolic Geometry. Connecting special values of Dedekind zeta functions $\zeta_K(2)$ to the Bloch–Wigner dilogarithm and hyperbolic 3-manifold volumes.

https://shmakov.us/docs/Slides/Siegel.pdf, Introduction to Siegel Modular Forms. Slides covering $\operatorname{Sp}_{2g}(\mathbb{Z})$, the Siegel upper half-space $\mathbb{H}_g$, Fourier–Jacobi expansions, and Hecke operators.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/benci-slides.pdf, Stochastic ODEs via Nonstandard Analysis. Elementary solutions to SDEs using hyperfinite random walks and nonstandard analysis.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/cutland-slides.pdf, Navier-Stokes via Loeb Measures. Solving the 3D stochastic Navier–Stokes equations using Loeb measure spaces.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/tsaban-slides.pdf, Superfilters and Ramsey Theory. Applications of ultrafilters and idempotent ultrafilters to combinatorial Ramsey theory.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/serpe-slides.pdf, Nonstandard Algebraic Geometry. Ultrafilters and nonstandard methods applied to scheme theory.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/velickovic-slides.pdf, Set Theory of Stone-Cech Compactifications. Set-theoretic structure of $\beta\mathbb{N} \setminus \mathbb{N}$ and ultrafilter spaces.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/vernaeve-slides.pdf, Ultrafilters and Generalized Functions. Constructing non-linear generalized functions via ultrafilter regularizations.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/linnocente-slides.pdf, Ultraproducts of Lie Algebras. Model theory and ultraproduct constructions for infinite-dimensional Lie algebras.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/dasgupta-slides.pdf, Partial Functions and Projective Limits. Topological aspects of partial functions and projective limits.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/blass-slides.pdf, Ultrafilters in Set Theory. Andreas Blass on cardinal characteristics of the continuum and ultrafilter properties.

https://people.dm.unipi.it/ultramath/slides/junge-slides.pdf, Ultraproducts in Operator Algebras. Marius Junge on ultraproducts of $C^*$-algebras and von Neumann factors.

https://stats385.github.io/assets/lectures/bolcskei-stats385-slides.pdf, Harmonic Analysis of Convolutional Neural Networks. 114 slides on scattering transforms, deformation stability, and wavelet frame representations in deep learning.

http://luthuli.cs.uiuc.edu/~daf/courses/CV23/Slides/lec04_frequency-daf.pdf, Frequency Analysis in Computer Vision. Gentle introduction to 2D Fourier transforms and spatial frequency filtering.

https://wwwpub.zih.tu-dresden.de/~dipa615c/pdf/talk-aims10-dresden.pdf, Hodge-Helmholtz Decompositions in Sobolev Spaces. Decomposing vector fields in weighted Sobolev spaces on exterior domains.
